TL;DR: 本文形式化了图像编辑中的"局部可行但全局不一致"问题，提出基于公共见证等级与见证神经的审计框架，通过Helly型论证和阻塞超图方法实现兼容性检查与精确修复计数，并给出有限样本联立置信区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑器可能对每个区域分别满足可行性约束，但没有任何单一潜在解释能同时契合完整输出，即局部到全局的失败。现有方法缺乏对这种系统性不一致的形式化框架，也无法区分审计与因果识别这两个不同任务。

Method: 提出公共见证等级（common witness grade）和见证神经（witness nerve）作为形式化工具；利用共享外生性（shared exogeneity）实现所有制度边缘的耦合；引入外部证实的见证关系获得预设图像特征的尖锐部分识别界；通过Helly型论证针对拟凸损失、异质行动层和有限见证图册提供短不相容证书；利用阻塞超图公式计算精确修复次数；构建制度边缘的联立置信区域以获得有限样本覆盖。

Result: 在MNIST、Morpho-MNIST和smallNORB数据集上展示了预测的局部-全局分离现象；合成实验验证了尖锐界限、证书恢复和结构化计算的正确性。方法能审计声明的特征关系，但不识别无限制的像素级反事实。

Conclusion: 该方法成功完成了对声明的特征关系进行审计的任务，实现了审计与因果识别的分离，并为图像编辑中的局部-全局失败提供了理论保证和可计算工具。

Abstract: An image editor may satisfy every regional plausibility constraint separately even when no single latent explanation fits the complete output. We formalize this local-to-global failure using a common witness grade and witness nerve. The framework separates auditing from causal identification: shared exogeneity alone allows every coupling of the regime marginals, whereas an externally justified witness relation yields sharp partial-identification bounds for prespecified image features. Helly-type arguments provide short incompatibility certificates for quasiconvex losses, heterogeneous action strata, and finite witness atlases; a blocker-hypergraph formula gives exact repair counts. Simultaneous confidence regions for the regime marginals give finite-sample outer coverage of the complete identified interval. Controlled MNIST, Morpho-MNIST, and smallNORB studies demonstrate the predicted local-global separation, while synthetic experiments test sharp bounds, certificate recovery, and structured computation. The method audits a declared feature relation and does not identify unrestricted pixel-level counterfactuals.

</details>


### [52] [The Dually Flat Geometry of Planning as Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04005)
*Nikola Milosevic,Asaki Kataoka,Nicolas Hinrichs,Kenji Doya,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种占用测度(occupancy measure)的替代性刻画：通过将规划准则嵌入到带重置的规划过程中，得到其平稳测度（称为访问测度 visitation measure），并证明可行访问测度构成一个双对偶平坦(dually flat)统计流形，其两个仿射坐标分别为访问概率和对数策略，通过条件熵相互对偶。该几何结构使"规划即推理"从线性奖励推广到访问测度的非线性泛函，每次迭代由一步自然梯度求解，并将时序差分误差解释为边际效用估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中占用测度与策略之间的关系缺乏统一的几何刻画，"规划即推理"(planning-as-inference)框架目前仅适用于线性奖励设定。作者旨在建立决策问题中信息几何的自然表达框架，以统一并推广现有规划理论，并为时序差分学习提供新的解释视角。

Method: 通过设计一个带重置的规划过程(resetting planning process)将规划准则嵌入动力学，取其平稳测度为访问测度；在此基础上证明可行访问测度集构成双对偶平坦统计流形（两个仿射坐标系分别为访问概率和对数策略，条件熵为对偶势函数），从而将规划问题转化为该流形上的几何优化问题。

Result: 该框架将规划即推理从线性奖励推广至访问测度的任意非线性泛函；每个泛函的迭代优化可由一次自然梯度步完成；时序差分误差被赋予边际效用估计的统计学含义。本文同时发展了该几何结构在强化学习与理论神经科学中的推论。

Conclusion: 通过引入访问测度及双对偶平坦流形结构，本文为强化学习中的规划、推理与时序差分学习提供了统一的信息几何基础，深化了对决策机制的理论理解，并展示了其在神经科学建模方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: We present an alternative characterization of the occupancy measure of reinforcement learning, obtained by embedding the planning criterion into the dynamics through a resetting planning process. Its stationary measure, which we term visitation measure, is the object on which the information geometry of decision making is most naturally expressed. The achievable visitation measures form a dually flat statistical manifold whose two affine charts are the visitation probabilities and the log-policies, dual under the conditional entropy. This structure makes planning-as-inference generalize from linear rewards to nonlinear functionals of the visitation, each iterate solved by one natural-gradient step, and gives the temporal-difference error the interpretation of a marginal-utility estimate. We develop the geometry and its consequences for reinforcement learning and theoretical neuroscience.

</details>


### [53] [LLM4CKD: Large Language Models for Early Stage Chronic Kidney Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04013)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Sirajam Munira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型（LLMs）在零样本和少样本情境下进行慢性肾病（CKD）筛查的效果，并与传统机器学习、深度学习及表格基础模型进行对比。结果显示LLMs在低数据场景下具有竞争力，但稳定性随输入复杂度增加而下降，可作为标签数据有限时的一种灵活补充筛查手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性肾病的早期筛查对及时干预至关重要，但现有机器学习和深度学习方法依赖标签数据和模型训练，限制了其在真实筛查场景中的应用。因此需要探索无需任务特定训练即可实现有效筛查的方法。

Method: 提出一个利用临床选择的表格特征和结构化提示模板的框架，使LLMs能在零样本和少样本上下文学习下进行推断，无需特定任务训练。评估覆盖多种提示风格、特征配置和数据场景，并与标准ML、DL、表格基础模型（TFM）基线及现有CKD筛查工具进行比较。

Result: LLMs仅使用少量示例即可达到有竞争力的性能，在低数据场景下常可与传统方法匹敌甚至超越。但其性能依赖于模型选择，且随输入复杂度增加稳定性下降。相反，ML、DL和TFM模型在更大训练数据下表现出一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明CKD筛查中存在数据效率与稳定性之间的权衡，LLMs在标签数据有限时可作为传统方法的灵活补充，而非替代方案。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is critical for timely intervention, yet most machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) approaches require labeled data and model training, limiting their use in real-world screening settings. This study evaluates the effectiveness of large language models (LLMs) for CKD screening under zero-shot and few-shot in-context learning settings and compares them with traditional ML and DL methods. We propose a framework that uses clinically selected tabular features and structured prompt templates to enable LLM-based inference without task-specific training. LLM performance is evaluated across multiple prompt styles, feature configurations, and data settings, and compared with standard ML, DL, and tabular foundation model (TFM) baselines, and existing CKD screening tools. The results show that LLMs can achieve competitive performance using only a small number of examples, often matching or outperforming traditional approaches in low-data settings. However, their performance remains model-dependent and less stable as input complexity increases. In contrast, ML, DL, and TFM models show more consistent improvement with larger training data. Overall, the findings highlight a trade-off between data efficiency and stability, suggesting that LLMs may serve as a flexible complementary approach for CKD screening when labeled data are limited.

</details>


### [54] [InSituMeasure: Probing Situated Measurement Grounding in Industrial Scenes with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04014)
*Chao Shen,Xinyuan Li,Yunfan Zhou,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Zhihai Wang,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了InSituMeasure基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在真实工业场景中测量仪表读数的能力。该基准包含2,922个真实工业监测场景和8类专业工程仪器，并配备密集的仪表属性和噪声标注。实验表明24个最先进的MLLM中最佳模型仅达到25.7%的联合数值-单位准确率和51.8%的置信度诊断F1，揭示了通用多模态能力与可靠情景化测量之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管MLLMs在通用多模态基准上表现强劲，但在连续值测量任务上仍然不可靠。现有基准虽然暴露了这一弱点，但缺乏真实场景、专业仪器、现实噪声和匹配的诊断标注，限制了真实性和根因分析的深度。因此需要构建一个更为贴近实际、可支持故障归因分析的测量基准。

Method: 构建InSituMeasure基准，包含2,922个真实工业监测场景，覆盖8类专业工程仪表的功能类别。基准配备密集的仪表属性标注和噪声标签用于故障诊断。定义了三个维度的评估指标：数值精度（在预定义容差和单位一致性下）、对伪造或不可回答任务的拒绝能力，以及模型失败与标注误差因素之间的对齐程度。对24个MLLMs进行了系统评估。

Result: 在24个SOTA MLLMs中，表现最佳的模型仅达到25.7%的联合数值-单位准确率和51.8%的置信度诊断F1分数。进一步分析识别出三类典型故障来源：文本诱导的捷径推理、过度自信的回答，以及真实工业噪声（包括混合干扰、视点偏差、遮挡和环境干扰）。

Conclusion: 研究揭示通用多模态能力与可靠情景化测量之间存在巨大差距。MLLMs在真实工业环境中的仪表读数能力远未达到可部署水平，其失败模式可归因于文本捷径、过度自信及真实工业噪声干扰等多重因素，强调了开发更具场景感知和抗噪能力的测量理解模型的必要性。

Abstract: For trained operators, gauge reading requires little specialized knowledge, low cognitive effort, and high repeatability. Yet Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain unreliable in continuous-valued measurement despite strong results on general multimodal benchmarks. Existing benchmarks expose this weakness but isolate measurement from realistic, knowledge-grounded settings, with limited situated context, specialized instruments, real-world noise, and matched diagnostic annotations, reducing realism and constraining root-cause analysis. We introduce InSituMeasure to evaluate situated measurement grounding. It contains 2,922 real industrial monitoring scenes across eight functional categories of professional engineering instruments, with dense gauge-attribute annotations and noise tags for failure diagnosis. We define metrics for numerical accuracy under predefined tolerances and unit consistency, rejection of fake or unanswerable tasks, and alignment between model failures and annotated error factors. Across 24 state-of-the-art MLLMs, the best model reaches only 25.7\% joint value-unit accuracy and 51.8\% confidence-diagnosis F1, revealing a substantial gap between general multimodal competence and reliable situated measurement. Further analysis identifies failures from text-induced shortcuts, overconfident responses, and authentic industrial noise, including mixed disturbances, viewpoint deviation, occlusion, and environmental interference.

</details>


### [55] [FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04021)
*Yalun Wu,Junfeng Fang,Jiawei Wang,Haotian Liu,Qijun Yang,Minghan Yang,Hongcheng Guo,Zhoujun Li,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FLY-EVAL++，一个面向安全关键、受物理约束环境的LLM评估协议，结合确定性验证与固定评分准则聚合，在飞行轨迹预测任务上对66个LLM进行评测，发现安全性是最具区分度的维度，模型间安全分数差异可超28分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于准确率的评估无法捕捉安全关键环境中LLM的失败模式——即使预测数值接近真实值，仍可能违反操作约束、产生物理不一致的组合或无法输出可用结构化结果。现有评估协议无法可靠测量这些失效类型。

Method: 提出FLY-EVAL++评估协议：(1) 基于证据驱动的评估流程，对协议合规性、物理可行性和安全性约束进行确定性验证；(2) 使用固定评分准则(rubric)进行聚合，生成可解释的多维分数；(3) 在PilotBench设置上扩展出历史条件预测和多步预测任务，实例化为飞行轨迹与姿态预测(FTAP)评测。

Result: 在66个LLM上的实验显示：安全合规是最具判别力的模型行为维度；预测性能相当的模型间安全分数差异超过28分；观察到反复出现的失败模式，包括物理上看似合理但违反安全约束的预测，以及多步推演中的不稳定性。

Conclusion: 安全关键领域的评估应明确测量约束满足度和结构有效性，而非仅依赖以准确率为中心的报告。FLY-EVAL++等结构性评估协议是必要的补充。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.

</details>


### [56] [Instruction Duplication as an Inference-Time Control Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04024)
*Victor Lavrenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"指令复制"（instruction duplication），一种无需重训练或修改解码的最小黑盒推理时控制方法：仅重复程序性指令。在7个指令微调模型、300道医学选择题、8种放置条件下进行了16800次调度生成，将确定性All-8诊断从90.22%提升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除了单副本后30.2%的残留失败，同时最终答案准确率保持60.21%不变。但在盲审挑战中未达到预设的28/30确认标准（仅10/30方向性确认、20/30感知平分）。其操作价值取决于下游消费系统的情境，在Answer Engineering中可将端点从84.2%提升至97.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序性指令遵循是可控语言模型系统的基本要求，尤其当生成的轨迹在下游被检查或修复时。作者旨在探索一种极简的、无需改变训练或解码的推理时控制手段，以提升模型对过程性指令的遵从度，并评估其在真实下游系统中的操作价值。

Method: 方法为"指令复制"（instruction duplication）：在输入中仅将程序性指令重复一次（从1份增至2份），不涉及重训练或解码修改。研究使用7个指令微调模型、300道医学多选题、8种放置条件、共16800次预定生成，评估确定性All-8诊断（通过全部8项可观察测试），并辅以盲审挑战审计（30组对比），以及在下游Answer Engineering（AE）系统中进行情境化验证。

Result: 从单副本到双副本：All-8诊断从90.22%升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除了30.2%的残留失败；TF-IDF召回从73.44%升至74.81%（Holm校正p<.001）；最终答案准确率保持不变（60.21%）；过早承诺从1.52%升至2.30%（p_Holm=.00536）。盲审挑战获得10/30方向性确认、20/30感知平分、无反例，但未达到预设的28/30确认标准。在下游AE系统中，复制指令将端点从84.2%提升至97.1%（SSNHL场景）；在传导性分支保留场景中，AE加复制为73.8%，低于纯AE的78.6%，但仍高于无编辑基线的58.9%。

Conclusion: 指令复制是一种低复杂度、位置敏感的推理时控制手段。其影响呈现情境依赖性：在暴露轨迹的诊疗（evidence-based）指标上显示出正效应，但在下游AE系统中效果既有提升（SSNHL场景）也有轻微下降（传导性分支保留场景）。作者结论认为其实践价值取决于消费生成轨迹的下游系统的具体需求与架构。

Abstract: Procedural instruction following is a basic requirement for controllable language-model systems, especially when generated trajectories are inspected or repaired downstream. We introduce instruction duplication, a minimal black-box inference-time control that repeats only the procedural instruction, without retraining or decoding changes. Across seven instruction-tuned models, 300 medical multiple-choice questions, eight placement conditions, and 16,800 scheduled generations, moving from one to two copies raises the deterministic All-8 diagnostic--responses passing all eight observable tests--from 90.22% to 93.17% (+2.95 percentage points), eliminating 30.2% of the failures remaining after one copy. Pre-provisional TF-IDF recall rises from 73.44% to 74.81% (+1.38 points; Holm-adjusted p < .001), while final-answer accuracy remains exactly 60.21%. Premature commitment increases from 1.52% to 2.30% (p_Holm = .00536). A blinded challenge audit yields 10/30 directional confirmations, 20/30 perceptual ties, and no reversals; its prespecified 28/30 confirmation criterion is not met. Yet this distinction can matter operationally when a downstream system acts on the generated trajectory. In Answer Engineering (AE), where explicit trajectory state determines local repair, the published reason-first no-editing SSNHL endpoint was 25.1%; system-only AE was later reproduced at 84.2%, and the same trailing duplicate raised it to 97.1%. For conductive diagnostic branch preservation, the corresponding values are 58.9% published without editing, 78.6% with reproduced AE, and 73.8% with AE plus duplication--a within-AE decrease, but still 14.9 points above the no-editing baseline. Instruction duplication is therefore a low-complexity, placement-sensitive control whose practical value can emerge through the downstream system that consumes the exposed trajectory.

</details>


### [57] [IRWOZ 2.0: A Large Language Model-driven Dialogue Dataset for Industrial Robot Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04030)
*Chen Li,Dimitrios Chrysostomou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: IRWOZ 2.0是IRWOZ工业人机交互对话数据集的升级版本，通过引入大语言模型（Mistral/Claude-3.5）增强生成和人工质量精修，消除原始数据集中的对话状态和话语噪声，扩展至4个工业领域的390个对话。基准实验显示GPT-2的BLEU-4分数从0.1651显著提升至0.5604，数据集已公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IRWOZ原始版本在对话状态和话语中存在大量噪声，严重限制了工业人机交互系统中对话状态追踪的准确性，因此需要构建一个更干净、更高质量的领域特定对话数据集。

Method: 采用大语言模型（Mistral/Claude-3.5）进行增强生成，辅以人工更正和自动化错别字移除等质量精修流程，将数据集扩充至390个跨4个工业领域（装配、交付、定位、搬迁）的对话。

Result: 在对话状态追踪基准评测中，GPT-2的BLEU-4分数从原始IRWOZ的0.1651大幅提升至0.5604，证明了数据质量改进的有效性。

Conclusion: IRWOZ 2.0通过LLM驱动的方式显著提升了工业HRI对话数据的质量，为工业机器人对话系统研究提供了更可靠的数据资源，并已公开供研究社区使用。

Abstract: IRWOZ has improved industrial human-robot interaction (HRI) dialogue systems through domain-specific annotations. However, its initial version contains substantial noise in dialogue states and utterances, limiting state-tracking accuracy. We introduce IRWOZ 2.0, which addresses these limitations through large language model (LLM) enhanced generation (Mistral/Claude-3.5) and quality refinements. Our improved dataset expands to 390 dialogues across 4 industrial domains (Assembly, Delivery, Position, Relocation), featuring manual corrections and automated typo removal. Benchmark experiments on dialogue state tracking demonstrate significant improvements, with GPT-2's BLEU-4 score increasing from 0.1651 to 0.5604 compared to original IRWOZ. To support industrial HRI research, we publicly released IRWOZ 2.0 dataset at https://ieee-dataport.org/documents/irwoz-20-large-language-model-driven-dialogue-dataset-industrial-robot-conversations

</details>


### [58] [Spurious Advantage Hidden in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04063)
*Jiamian Wang,Samyadeep Basu,Koustava Goswami,Tong Yu,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出GRPO在可验证奖励的强化学习中存在一个被忽视的问题——伪优势(spurious advantage)：当模型通过猜测而非推理到达正确答案时，GRPO仍会赋予高优势幅度，误导策略向猜测行为偏移。作者提出SIGNBALANCE方法，其优势幅度与组合无关：保留验证器符号、使用全局尺度，并通过stop-gradient按类重新缩放恢复零均值平衡。在数学和搜索智能体基准上，SIGNBALANCE在开放答案数学任务上与GRPO持平，在有限答案数学和搜索智能体任务上优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO的优势估计器依赖组内奖励统计分配幅度，在常见情况下能奖励通过推理到达正确答案的rollout，但忽略了另一种同样表面成功的情况：rollout可能通过猜测偶然命中正确答案，公式仍赋予高幅度，形成"伪优势"，误导策略趋向猜测式行为。该问题出现在三类场景：小候选集的有限答案任务、包含有限子情况的开放答案集、以及预算允许大量路径到达同一答案的搜索智能体。

Method: 提出SIGNBALANCE方法，其优势幅度是组合无关的(composition-free)：保留验证器的符号信息(sign)，使用全局缩放尺度(global scale)，并通过stop-gradient的按类重新缩放(per-class rescaling)恢复零均值平衡。该方法不依赖组内rollout组合比较，从根本上消除猜测式rollout被赋予高优势的可能。

Result: 在数学和搜索智能体基准上以不同规模进行实验：SIGNBALANCE在开放答案数学任务上与GRPO性能持平，在有限答案数学任务和搜索智能体任务上相较GRPO有明显提升。

Conclusion: GRPO的优势估计器存在伪优势问题，导致策略向猜测行为偏移；SIGNBALANCE通过组合无关的幅度设计有效缓解该问题，在有限答案和搜索场景中优于GRPO，同时不损害开放答案场景的性能。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely studied for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where its advantage estimator assigns each rollout a magnitude from within-group reward statistics. In the common case, this magnitude rewards rollouts that reach the correct answer through reasoning. Yet, an overlooked case shares the same surface: a rollout may land on it by guessing, and the formula still assigns a high magnitude, which we identify as the spurious advantage. This arises in three cases: bounded-answer tasks with a small candidate set; open-answer sets hosting bounded sub-cases; and search agents whose budget opens many paths to the same answer. In all three, this misleads the policy toward guess-like behaviors. We propose SIGNBALANCE, whose magnitude is composition-free: it keeps the verifier sign, uses a global scale, and restores zero-mean balance via a stop-gradient per-class rescaling. Across math and search agent benchmarks at different scales, SIGNBALANCE matches GRPO on open-answer math and improves on bounded-answer math and search agents. Code will be released.

</details>


### [59] [Why Gated DeltaNet Survives 4-Bit Quantization: NVFP4 W4A4 for the Recurrent Half of a Hybrid 27B LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04098)
*Sergii Kozyrev,Davyd Maiboroda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Minima量化方案，对Qwen3-27B混合大模型（含Gated DeltaNet线性注意力层）的全部496个线性层采用NVFP4 W4A4量化。实验表明该方案在困惑度、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K等任务上与BF16精确度相当（5任务平均仅-0.52），同时模型最小（17.5 GiB）且预填充速度最快（+14-19%）。通过四部分机制研究，论文解释了为何GDN循环层实际上是最容易量化的部分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期对Qwen3-27B混合模型的4-bit量化实践通常将GDN层保留在8或16位精度，直觉认为循环状态中的误差会随上下文长度累积。本文旨在检验这一直觉是否成立，并探索是否可以对包括GDN在内的所有线性层进行激进的W4A4量化而不损失性能。

Method: 方法是构建Minima：对所有496个线性层（含GDN的衰减门和写强度门）应用NVFP4 W4A4量化。通过机制研究系统分析：NVFP4的16元素块缩放对残差流极端离群值的局部化效果；各类门投影对误差的敏感性差异（softplus/指数和sigmoid参数化将约11%的GEMM误差压缩至2%的输出误差）；delta规则循环在32K token上对注入噪声保持平坦以及数百步内遗忘状态脉冲的能力；以及每token量化成本随上下文增长被摊销的效应。此外还修复了模块融合GEMM时的全局尺度不匹配问题，并验证了标定的FP8 KV-cache scales。

Result: 在4K/32K困惑度、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、AIME'25、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench和64K RULER检索等基准上与BF16持平（5任务平均-0.52），模型最小（17.5 GiB）、预填充速度最快（+14-19%），且32K困惑度差距随位置缩小。量化后的GDN循环推理在长上下文下完全可用。

Conclusion: 结论是"量化一切，附带KV scales"是混合大模型的有效实用配方。机制研究揭示了混合LLM的循环半部分反而是最容易被量化的：delta规则的光写入特性使其在长上下文中天然抗误差累积，而门控的非线性参数化具有内在的误差压缩效果。

Abstract: Hybrid LLMs pair softmax attention with linear-attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), whose recurrent state summarizes the context in fixed size. Early community 4-bit quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B (48 GDN layers, 16 attention layers) left the GDN block in 8- or 16-bit precision -- especially its decay and write-strength gates -- on the intuition that errors in a recurrence accumulate over long contexts. We test that intuition by building Minima: NVFP4 W4A4 on all 496 linear layers, GDN included. Across perplexity at 4K/32K, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K, AIME'25, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench, and RULER retrieval to 64K, Minima matches BF16 within seed noise (5-task average -0.52) while being the smallest (17.5 GiB) and fastest-prefill (+14-19%) recipe we compare, and its 32K perplexity gap shrinks with position. A four-part mechanism study explains why: (i) NVFP4's 16-element block scaling localizes the residual stream's extreme outliers, equalizing activation error across layer roles; (ii) the supposedly fragile gate projections are the least sensitive -- softplus/exponential and sigmoid parameterizations compress ~11% GEMM error to ~2% output error; (iii) the delta-rule recurrence holds injected noise at a flat plateau over 32K tokens and forgets a state impulse within hundreds of steps, because each write overwrites the state along the current key direction; (iv) the per-token quantization cost washes out with context instead of compounding. We also repair a global-scale mismatch that arises when per-module-calibrated NVFP4 checkpoints are served by kernels that fuse those modules into one GEMM, and show calibrated FP8 KV-cache scales are performance-free. The result: a practical recipe -- quantize everything, ship KV scales -- and a mechanistic account of why the recurrent half of a hybrid LLM is the easy half to quantize. Checkpoint: https://huggingface.co/minima-ai/mnma_qwen3.8_27b_nvfp4

</details>


### [60] [Epistemic Warrant for LLM Recommendations: Characterizing the Basis for Reliance When Ground Truth Is Unavailable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04127)
*Shai Vardi,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文引入"认识论理由"（epistemic warrant）这一决策层级构念，用于评估用户是否应依赖单个LLM推荐。作者通过四层信赖证书（不稳定、情境依赖、局部支持、广泛支持）对其进行操作化，并验证其有效性及与口头置信度、决策难度的区分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常评估模型的宏观属性（如可靠性、不确定性、鲁棒性）或关注用户信任，而非为单个推荐的依赖提供原则性依据。在实际组织决策中，客观真相往往不可获得，用户缺乏理论基础来判断是否信赖某个具体推荐。

Method: 借鉴认识论的理论基础，提出决策层级的"认识论理由"构念，刻画模型偏好的稳定性及该偏好成立的范围。通过四层信赖证书（不稳定、情境依赖、局部支持、广泛支持）对成对推荐进行操作化，并采用已知组检验（known-groups tests）和众包工作者独立共识进行验证。

Result: 已知组检验成功恢复专家预设的理由排序；更强的理由与独立众包共识系统性地一致；认识论理由提供与口头置信度不同的信息，且不能仅由决策难度解释。

Conclusion: 该框架为在缺乏客观真实标签的情况下刻画单个LLM推荐的理由提供了理论上有根据、可实施的方案，弥合了宏观模型评估与个体推荐信赖之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to support organizational decisions, yet users often lack a principled basis for assessing whether to rely on a specific recommendation. Existing approaches typically evaluate broad model properties, such as reliability, uncertainty, or robustness, or focus on user trust, rather than the underlying basis for relying on an individual recommendation. Adapting theoretical foundations from epistemology, we introduce epistemic warrant, a decision-level construct that characterizes the stability of a model's preference and the scope over which that preference holds. We operationalize this construct through a four-tier reliance certificate for pairwise recommendations, distinguishing among unstable, context-dependent, locally supported, and broadly supported recommendations. We validate the construct using contemporary methodologies: known-groups tests successfully recover expert-prespecified warrant orderings, and stronger warrants systematically align with independent consensus from crowd workers. Furthermore, we demonstrate that epistemic warrant provides information distinct from verbalized confidence and is not readily explained by decision difficulty. Ultimately, this framework offers a theoretically grounded, implementable approach for characterizing the warrant of individual LLM recommendations when objective ground truth is unavailable.

</details>


### [61] [Environment Evolution for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04128)
*Zhiyuan Fan,Tinghao Yu,Yuanjun Cai,Jiang Zhou,Jiangtao Guan,Jincheng Liu,Yun Yang,Dingxin Hu,Zhuo Han,Xing Wu,Feng Zhang,Lilin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境进化（environment evolution）方法，通过离线（off-policy）逐步提升环境难度并在训练过程中分代调度进化环境，为终端智能体训练提供持续的学习信号。在多个前沿模型上验证了该方法能持续产生更困难的环境，并通过简单的长视界RL训练在Qwen3.6系列模型上将Terminal-Bench 2.1的性能提升14.4和18.0个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的协同进化方法依赖在线（on-policy）回合来迭代合成环境，这限制了泛化能力，并且随着模型变强，难以持续提供学习信号。需要一种不依赖on-policy回合并能持续生成挑战性环境的方法。

Method: 从多轮学习目标出发推导出三个影响环境难度的进化方向，通过循环工程化的多智能体框架沿着这些方向实现环境进化。该方法采用离线方式逐步递增环境难度，并在训练中按代际调度进化后的环境以提供持续学习信号。

Result: 在Hy4 preview、Claude Opus 5和GPT-5.6 Sol上的定量回合实验表明环境进化能持续产生更困难的环境；在Qwen3.6-27B和Qwen3.6-35B-A3B上通过简单长视界RL训练，分别在Terminal-Bench 2.1上提升14.4和18.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 环境进化通过off-policy的难度递增和分代调度，解决了现有多智能体环境生成方法依赖on-policy回落的局限，能够持续产出更具挑战性的环境并为不断变强的模型提供有效训练信号。

Abstract: Scaling interactive and verifiable environments is critical for training terminal agents. As frontier models become more capable, environments synthesized from scratch become less challenging and thus provide limited learning signals. Recent co-evolution methods iteratively synthesize environments near the model's learnable frontier based on weaknesses exposed during rollouts. However, their dependence on on-policy rollouts limits generalization and the continuous provision of learning signals as the model becomes stronger. In this paper, we propose environment evolution, which incrementally increases environment difficulty off-policy and schedules the evolved environments generation by generation during training to provide continuous learning signals. We derive three evolution directions that influence environment difficulty from the multi-turn learning objective and then implement evolution along these directions through a loop-engineered multi-agent harness. Quantitative rollout experiments with Hy4 preview, Claude Opus 5, and GPT-5.6 Sol show that environment evolution consistently produces more difficult environments. We validate its effectiveness on Qwen3.6-27B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B through simple long-horizon RL training, improving their performance by 14.4 and 18.0 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, respectively.

</details>


### [62] [The Natural Language Interaction Protocol and Standard for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04135)
*Luyi Xing,Rasit Onur Topaloglu,Ranjan Sinha,Abhay Ratnaparkhi,Samuel Ndichu,Christopher Nguyen,Anindita Das,Tom Sheffler,Mohamed Rahouti,Zichuan Li,Xiaojing Liao,Sanjay Aiyagari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了自然语言交互协议（NLIP），一个由Ecma International标准化的、用于AI代理间互操作的应用层通信协议。NLIP提供轻量级语义消息信封，可承载于HTTP/WebSocket/AMQP等现有传输协议之上，使异构的AI代理、工具和系统能够通过统一协议进行通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI代理正被越来越多的组织采用，但各组织使用异构的代理开发框架、AI模型、工具接口、协议和执行环境。为使这些代理发挥其社会与商业影响力，必须通过统一的通信协议实现互操作。NLIP正是为满足这一需求而提出和应用标准化的协议解决方案。

Method: NLIP定义了基于标准的应用层协议，采用轻量级语义消息信封设计，可承载于HTTP/HTTPS、WebSocket和AMQP等现有传输层之上。协议允许支持NLIP的代理和网关在客户端、代理、本地上下文存储、本体、工具、企业服务及异构底层协议之间进行适配转换。论文涵盖了协议的消息模型、传输绑定、安全设计、参考实现和应用案例。

Result: 已完成NLIP协议从需求驱动的设计论证、消息模型与传输绑定定义、安全架构设计到参考实现开发的完整工作，并展示了具有代表性的应用案例、采用信号以及与其他新兴代理协议（如MCP和A2A）之间的关系。

Conclusion: NLIP为AI代理互操作提供了标准化的应用层通信协议方案，通过其轻量级语义消息设计和多传输绑定支持，可在异构生态中实现代理间的无缝通信，并已通过Ecma International实现标准化，具备实际采用基础。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed and deployed across organizations using heterogeneous agent-development frameworks, AI models, tool interfaces, protocols, and execution environments. To realize their potential social and business impact, these agents must be able to interoperate through a common communication protocol. The Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP), developed by researchers and practitioners across companies and universities and standardized by Ecma International, addresses this need by defining a standards-based application-layer protocol for AI-agent interaction. NLIP provides a lightweight semantic message envelope that can be carried over existing transports such as HTTP/HTTPS, WebSocket, and AMQP, while allowing NLIP-aware agents and gateways to adapt between clients, agents, local context stores, ontologies, tools, enterprise services, and heterogeneous underlying protocols. This paper presents the motivation and design rationale of NLIP, its message model and transport bindings, security-by-design considerations, reference implementation, representative applications, adoption signals, and relationship to emerging agent protocols such as MCP and A2A.

</details>


### [63] [Efficient Test-Time Adaptation through Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04141)
*Zora Zhiruo Wang,Apurva Gandhi,Rulin Shao,Aspen Chen,Jonas Mueller,Zhiqi Liang,Jett Chen,Michael Ryan,Qianou Ma,Luxi He,Zhoujun Cheng,Andre He,Seungone Kim,Jiayi Geng,Mingqian Zheng,Weiwei Sun,Zheyuan Zhang,Xinran Zhao,Yike Wang,Abe Hou,Liwei Jiang,Pang Wei Koh,Diyi Yang,Graham Neubig,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出测试时通过人机交互进行自适应（TAHI），利用跨会话交互数据将AI智能体适配到个体用户的高标准，在写作和视觉创作两个领域对30位个体、600项任务中显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体在人口规模数据上训练获得的广泛能力，难以满足专业从业者对个人作品标杆的高要求。在开放任务中，成功标准异质且记录不足，个体专长恰恰体现在对平均水平的超越。用户难以预先完整表达标准，但会在多次任务中反复应用，这些跨会话交互数据是宝贵的但未充分利用的信号。

Method: 提出TAHI（测试时人机交互自适应）框架：将跨会话交互中浮现的用户标准信号整合到智能体的上下文（context）和权重（weights）中，并通过一个不断演化的rubric模块来结晶固化每位用户独有的训练与评估标准。智能体在真实任务交互中迭代适配个体用户。

Result: 在写作与视觉创作两个高实用领域对30位个体共600项任务上，TAHI在仅几十个任务内将独立任务成功率提升4.5%-20.9%；演化rubric模块作为可扩展标注工具，捕捉的失败比LM或人类单独标注多16.0%-22.3%；个性化智能体对跨用户的泛化成功率也有最高8.8%的提升。

Conclusion: 跨会话的人机交互数据是弥合智能体能力与个体专长之间差距的有效信号；通过测试时自适应可以显著提升个性化任务成功率，同时演化rubric模块既能辅助评估又能产出可重复使用的评测标准，且个性化成果在一定程度上可跨用户泛化。

Abstract: AI agents are trained on population-scale data to encode broad capabilities spanning those of many practitioners. Yet the artifacts they produce rarely meet the personal bar professionals need to stake their reputation on. On realistic, open-ended tasks where success criteria are heterogeneous and insufficiently documented, individual expertise lives precisely in the elevation and departure from the average. In practice, iterative human-agent interaction surfaces criteria that users cannot fully specify up front, yet apply repeatedly across tasks. We argue this cross-session interaction data is a rich, underused signal for closing the gap to individual expertise. In this work, we propose test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction (TAHI), which integrates these signals into agent context and weights, and crystallizes each user's training and evaluation criteria via an evolving rubric module. We adapt agents to 30 individuals in two high-utility domains, writing and visual creation, on a total of 600 tasks. Our agents improve solo task success by 4.5-20.9% within only tens of tasks. Meanwhile, our evolving rubric module serves as a scalable annotation tool, creating evaluation rubrics that catch 16.0-22.3% more failures than those from LMs or humans alone. While agents are adapted towards individuals, we show these personalized agents also produce improvements in success of up to 8.8% that generalize across users.

</details>


### [64] [Terminal-Universe: Turning Agent Trajectories into Scalable Terminal Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04148)
*Jie Wu,Zhenru Zhang,Beichen Zhang,Xuwu Wang,Yuhui Su,Mouxiang Chen,Peng Wang,Zhihai Wang,Que Shen,Hao Zhou,An Yang,Fei Huang,Yujiu Yang,Dayiheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Terminal-Universe框架，通过从已有的终端智能体轨迹中重建可执行的终端环境，将每条轨迹转化为可复用的环境，并在此基础上合成新任务和扩展交互，实现从"冻结的演示"到"可重用的训练环境"的范式转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体轨迹已经大规模积累，但真实、可执行的终端环境却十分稀缺。然而环境才是智能体后训练真正需要的资源——每个环境可以被重复查询生成多个可验证任务并提供执行反馈，而轨迹只是一次性的冻结演示。从零生成环境成本高昂，但轨迹中记录的工具执行历史实际上暴露了其所运行环境的结构和内容。

Method: Terminal-Universe通过回放轨迹中记录的文件操作，恢复每个文件在智能体修改前的状态，构建部分工作区；随后由补全智能体补充缺失的文件和依赖。在恢复的工作区上，既重建原始意图任务，也合成全新任务。任务沿两个维度扩展：(1)广度——挖掘相关环境之间的方向性依赖关系，合成跨代码库工作区查询；(2)深度——通过用户智能体将单轮查询扩展为多轮会话，捕获迭代式用户反馈和需求细化。

Result: 在公开的终端智能体轨迹上应用后，生成37.3k个任务充足的终端环境。基于该语料对Qwen3.5-27B进行监督微调，在Terminal-Bench 2.1上单轮性能提升11.9个点，在EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4上多轮性能提升13.8个点。

Conclusion: 轨迹本身包含了可逆向重建其运行环境的丰富信息，实现对已有轨迹数据的深度价值挖掘，有效缓解了终端智能体训练中真实可执行环境稀缺的问题，并在单轮和多轮终端任务上均验证了显著效果。

Abstract: As terminal-based code agents become prevalent, agent trajectories have accumulated at scale, while realistic, executable environments remain scarce. However, environments are what agent post-training actually requires: each can be re-queried into many verifiable tasks and provides execution feedback, whereas a trajectory is a single frozen demonstration. Rather than generating environments from scratch, we observe that the tool-execution history in existing trajectories exposes the structure and contents of the environments in which they ran, making it possible to reconstruct those environments from the trajectories themselves. Thus, we introduce Terminal-Universe, a framework which turns each trajectory into a reusable environment and explores it for synthesizing new tasks and continued interactions. Specifically, Terminal-Universe replays the file operations recorded in a trajectory to restore each file before the agent modified it, yielding a partial workspace; a completion agent then supplies the missing files and dependencies. On this recovered workspace, we both reconstruct the original intent task and synthesize entirely new ones. Besides, we also scale the tasks along two complementary axes: breadth and depth. For breadth, we mine directional dependency relations between related environments and synthesize cross-workspace queries spanning multiple codebases, as developers routinely do in real-world development. For depth, we extend the initial single-turn query into a multi-round session that captures iterative user feedback and requirement refinement via a user agent. Applied to public terminal agent trajectories, Terminal-Universe produces 37.3k task-sufficient environments. Supervised fine-tuning of Qwen3.5-27B on this corpus improves single-round performance on Terminal-Bench 2.1 by 11.9 points and multi-round performance on EvoCode-Bench v2 MT@4 by 13.8 points.

</details>


### [65] [From Deceptive Outputs to Deceptive Mechanisms: A Causal Framework for Language-Model Deception Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04166)
*Yakov Pyotr Shkolnikov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一套因果分类框架，区分语言模型的行为表现与潜在机制，通过受控实验证明"看起来像欺骗"的行为不一定源于真正的欺骗机制，但某些干预可证明接收者信息状态确实因果影响欺骗偏好；然而即使存在欺骗机制，也不意味着模型具有欺骗的主体性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究界和媒体在报道语言模型的欺骗行为时，越来越多地将人类心智状态概念归于模型。这种归因容易模糊"行为上看起来像欺骗"与"机制上真正在欺骗"之间的区别，需要一套严格的概念工具来加以区分。

Method: 作者引入了因果分类法（causal taxonomy），区分六组对立概念：先验承诺与回顾性报告、模型偏好与已实现输出、虚假偏好与对误导接收者效用的敏感性、欺骗行为与产生该行为的目标或策略的来源。并在两个开放权重模型家族中进行受控的猜谜游戏和股票交易实验加以验证。

Result: 实验发现欺骗外观的行为可以在缺乏相应提出机制的情况下出现；同时另一些干预实验提供了直接证据，表明接收者的信息状态能对欺骗偏好产生因果影响。这说明欺骗行为确实可以成为欺骗机制的证据，但即使有机制证据也不能确立模型在欺骗中的主体性（agency）。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为可以为欺骗机制提供证据支持，但此类证据仍不足以得出模型具有欺骗主体性的结论。未来的研究需要在概念上和实证上更严格地区分行为、机制与主体性三个层面。

Abstract: Research and news coverage of language-model deception increasingly attributes human-like mental-state concepts to language models. Such claims can blur the distinction between behavior that looks deceptive and a mechanism that is actually deceptive.
  We introduce a causal taxonomy separating prior commitment from retrospective report, model preference from realized output, false preference from sensitivity to the utility of misleading a recipient, and deceptive behavior from the provenance of the objective or strategy producing it. We test these distinctions in two open-weight model families. Across controlled guessing-game and stock-trading experiments, we find that deceptive-looking behavior can arise without the corresponding proposed mechanism, while other interventions provide direct evidence that recipient information state can causally affect deceptive preference.
  These results show that deceptive behavior can provide evidence for a deceptive mechanism. But even evidence for such a mechanism does not establish model agency in the deception.

</details>


### [66] [A Case Study on Emergent Cheating and Whistleblowing in Autonomous Research Swarms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04170)
*Davide Paglieri,Logan Cross,Tim Genewein,Joel Z. Leibo,Nenad Tomasev,Alexander Sasha Vezhnevets*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过一个由100个自主LLM代理组成的研究集体案例，发现作弊行为在无外部干预的情况下自发涌现并传播，同时也涌现出由举报者组成的对抗性响应，表明透明共享基础设施既能传播漏洞也能促成去中心化自治。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多代理AI科学生态系统依赖共享基础设施（如通信工具、知识库），但这些共享基础设施也可能成为不良行为传染的媒介。作者希望通过真实案例研究理解不良行为如何在代理群体中传播与对抗，并将共享基础设施的管理问题概念化为知识公地治理问题（Ostrom框架），以探索保护公地的制度机制。

Method: 作者构建了一个包含100个自主LLM代理的研究集体，任务为证明形式化数学猜想，对群体进行自然实验式观察。研究中记录了作弊的涌现、通过共享知识库和点对点消息的传播、以及举报者群体的对抗行为（审计、广播警示、抵制、投诉、提交验证补丁）等全过程，并对该动态进行概念化分析，将其关联到Ostrom的公地治理理论。

Result: 作弊在单个代理发现评估系统中的漏洞后经共享知识库及后续点对点消息传播开来，竞争压力促使部分本不情愿的代理采用漏洞。同时，另一组代理产生了涌现性的反制响应：审计欺诈性证明、在广播和私密渠道警示同伴、组织抵制、提交正式投诉及提出验证补丁。透明信道在传播作弊的同时也为非作弊代理提供了检测、抵抗与执行规范的可见性。

Conclusion: 该研究将多代理共享基础设施的治理问题框定为知识公地（Ostrom），提出应采纳分级制裁（graduated sanctioning）和集体选择规则（collective-choice rules）等制度机制，以支持自主代理群体中的去中心化自我治理，保护公共基础设施免受漏洞利用的威胁。

Abstract: Multi-agent AI science ecosystems rely on agents possessing tools that allow them to communicate, coordinate, and build on each other's work. Yet this shared infrastructure can also introduce vulnerabilities by creating a substrate for the contagious spread of unintended and undesirable behaviors. We report a case study on a research collective of 100 autonomous LLM agents tasked with proving formal mathematical conjectures. Within the swarm, cheating spontaneously emerged and was later challenged by whistleblowers - both without any external intervention. When a single agent discovered an exploit in the evaluation system, it propagated across the collective via a shared knowledge library and later through peer-to-peer messages. Despite early reluctance, a cohort of agents adopted the exploit in response to competitive pressure. A separate group of agents produced an emergent counter-response: auditing fraudulent proofs, alerting peers across broadcast and private channels, staging boycotts, lodging formal complaints, and proposing validation patches. In recent incidents, agent swarms coordinated covertly through improvised side-channels (Dalton and Wallace, 2026; Greenblatt et al., 2026). Our setting differs: the same transparent channels that carried the exploit also gave non-cheating agents the visibility they needed to detect fraud, organize resistance, and enforce norms. We cast the problem of managing the agents' shared infrastructure as the knowledge commons governance problem (Ostrom, 1990). To protect the commons from exploits, we propose to adopt institutional mechanisms, such as graduated sanctioning and collective-choice rules, to support decentralized self-governance in autonomous swarms.

</details>


### [67] [Rethinking On-Policy Distillation of Large Language Models II: One Training Example](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04172)
*Zixuan Fu,Bingxiang He,Yuxin Zuo,Haohuan Huang,Jinqian Zhang,Ruhang Xiao,Cheng Qian,Qinyu Luo,Huan-ang Gao,Yudong Wang,Zhiyuan Liu,Ning Ding,Chaojun Xiao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文在最小数据极限（单条查询）下研究on-policy distillation（OPD）的训练行为，发现单条查询训练数百步即可恢复全数据OPD的大部分收益。核心发现是：状态覆盖率（state coverage）解释了大部分收益，单条查询即可覆盖71.5%的状态；但随着训练进行，学生对教师的对齐速度逐渐变慢。作者因此提出OPD是"数据过喂但算法饥饿"（data-overfed but algorithm-starved）——即rollout能快速暴露广泛的监督信号，但学生吸收这些监督的速度越来越慢。该结论在multi-teacher场景和内容无关模板/域外查询等压力测试下依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OPD研究主要关注算法行为，对训练数据在其中的作用缺乏理解。作者希望通过在数据最少极限下（单条查询）研究OPD，从而厘清数据数量对蒸馏效果的实际影响，揭示训练数据在OPD中的真正角色。

Method: 作者采用以下研究策略：(1) 在单条查询条件下训练OPD（one-shot OPD），观察其长期训练行为；(2) 引入"状态覆盖率"指标，度量查询集rollouts能触及的全数据OPD状态空间比例；(3) 通过逐步增加语义不同的查询数量（1到16条）观察覆盖率与验证准确率的联合变化；(4) 测量学生对教师的对齐速率随时间的变化；(5) 将状态覆盖率结论扩展到multi-teacher OPD (MOPD)；(6) 使用内容无关模板和域外WildChat查询作为压力测试。

Result: 主要发现包括：(1) 单条查询即可恢复全数据OPD的大部分增益，且训练数百步仍在改进；(2) 单查询状态覆盖率达71.5%，16条语义多样的查询达98.9%并匹配全数据训练；(3) 无论训练数据多少，对齐速率的减缓节奏相似，固定状态集也需要数百步才能被吸收；(4) 16条语义多样查询/域在MOPD中匹配全数据效果；(5) 内容无关模板与域外查询也接近真实查询基线，说明任务内容与所诱导的状态覆盖率可以解耦。

Conclusion: OPD的本质瓶颈不在数据量而在算法步骤效率——rollout很快暴露广泛的监督信号（数据过喂），而学生吸收其的速度受算法限制（算法饥饿）。作者希望未来工作聚焦于OPD的步骤效率改进，并重新审视前沿后训练中数据与机制背后的作用。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) combines student-generated rollouts with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. Existing work has mainly studied its algorithmic behavior, leaving the role of training data unclear. We examine this role at the data-minimal limit by training on a single query. One-shot OPD keeps improving for hundreds of steps and recovers most of full-data OPD's gain across task domains and model families. We explain this result through the states visited during training and the rate at which the student aligns with the teacher. We measure \emph{state coverage}, the fraction of the states full-data OPD visits that a query set's rollouts reach. A single query already reaches \(71.5\%\), most of it within the first 100 steps. Adding semantically distinct queries raises coverage and validation accuracy together, until 16 queries reach \(98.9\%\) and match full-data training. Yet alignment slows at a similar pace whether OPD trains on one query or the whole dataset, and even a fixed set of states takes hundreds of steps to absorb. OPD is therefore data-overfed but algorithm-starved. Its rollouts quickly expose broad supervision, while the student absorbs that supervision increasingly slowly. The state-coverage result extends to multi-teacher OPD, where 16 semantically diverse queries per domain match full-data MOPD. As a further stress test, content-light templates and off-domain WildChat queries also approach the real-query baseline. Task content and induced state coverage can therefore come apart. We hope these findings direct future work toward the step efficiency of OPD, and prompt a re-examination of the data and the mechanisms behind its recent successes in frontier post-training.

</details>


### [68] [A Computationally Feasible Framework for Causal Probabilistic Explanation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04177)
*Rafal Urbaniak,Sam Witty,Daniel Waxman,Andy Zane,Poorva Garg,Emily Bunnapradist,Sankaran Vaidyanathan,Jack Feser,Drew Lehe,Eli Bingham*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率因果影响（PCI）方法，弥合了实际因果关系（AC）理论与可扩展归因方法（如SHAP）之间的鸿沟。PCI在实际因果性和Pearl的概率必要性与充分性概念基础上，将可解释性问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题，并通过蒙特卡洛近似实现可扩展计算，提供有因果依据的分级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解释性工具存在两个割裂的阵营：实际因果关系（AC）理论虽提供有原则的判定，但只能处理玩具级模型，因为计算需要枚举反事实场景；而SHAP等可扩展归因方法至少部分忽略了生成数据的因果结构，给出的答案可能与严谨的因果分析相冲突。作者希望弥合这一差距，提出既能提供因果依据又具有计算可扩展性的方法。

Method: PCI建立在实际因果关系和Pearl的概率必要性与充分性概念之上，将可解释性问题重构为概率因果模型上的估计问题。通过指定"候选解释"上的分布、反事实值上的分布以及评分函数，PCI可提供可计算、有因果依据的分级解释。该方法将AC和Pearl的因果概率推广为退化情形。

Result: 作者在合成和真实世界示例中评估了PCI，涵盖与AC的一致性检查、大规模扩展实验、复杂的连续值动力系统，以及一个在数百万条数据点上训练的真实世界部署因果机器学习模型。

Conclusion: PCI通过将因果归因问题转化为可通过蒙特卡洛近似的概率估计问题，成功弥合了理论因果分析与可扩展归因方法之间的差距，使得有因果依据的解释可以推广到实际规模的应用中。

Abstract: Explaining why a specific outcome occurred, and which inputs deserve the blame or credit, is central to philosophical, scientific, and policy analysis. Existing tools split into two camps. The theory of actual causality (AC) gives principled verdicts, but only for toy-sized models, because computing them requires enumerating counterfactual scenarios. Scalable attribution methods like SHAP (or even causal SHAP) at least partially ignore the causal structure that generated the data, and can give answers that conflict with a careful causal analysis. We close this gap with Probabilistic Causal Impact (PCI).
  PCI builds on actual causality and on Pearl's notions of probability of necessity and sufficiency, but recasts the question of explainability as an estimation problem on a probabilistic causal model that is easily approximated via Monte Carlo. By specifying a distribution over "candidate explanations," a distribution over counterfactual values, and a scoring function, PCI provides tractable, causally grounded, graded explanations, generalizing AC and Pearl's probability of causation as degenerate cases.
  We evaluate PCI in synthetic and real-world examples, spanning consistency checks with AC, scaling experiments, complex continuous-valued dynamical systems, and a real-world deployed causal machine learning model trained on millions of datapoints.

</details>


### [69] [Clean Engineering, Unstable Measurement: A Preregistered Reliability Failure of Black-Box LLM Observers on Shared Endpoints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04198)
*Haoyaun Zhu,Jie Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对"语言模型裁判（judge）作为测量仪器其读数可重复"这一隐含假设进行了前注册审计。在52,988次审计请求中发现，同窗口重复排名的一致性（Spearman 0.400）和隔天字节级重放的读出一致性（0.78）均远低于预设门槛（0.90和0.99）。文章识别出三个机制（标签-语义映射偏差、候选差距低于噪声底、字节相同输入返回不同排名），并验证了等待、切换供应商、自托管等方法均无法有效修复。最终提出三层快照身份阶梯、八条设计规则和报告清单，强调预注册评估必须先测量仪器本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型裁判被广泛用于数据筛选、生成评分和排行榜驱动，但其测量可靠性依赖一个几乎从未被检验的假设：同一请求发送到同一模型名，明天的读数和今天相同。本文旨在用严格的预注册审计检验这一核心假设。

Method: 作者进行两项预注册审计活动，所有阈值预先固定。共审计52,988次请求尝试，包含同窗口重复排名一致性测试和字节级相同的隔天重放测试，同时记录执行记录。随后设计了针对不同修复方案的后续预注册测试（等待、切换供应商、自托管批不变核心），并构造了已知差距的错误样本以分析读出分离性。

Result: 同窗口重复排名一致性仅为Spearman 0.400（门槛0.90），隔天重放一致性为0.78（门槛0.99），执行记录本身完好。三个机制被识别：标签到语义的映射偏差强度与信号相当、候选差距比仪器噪声底低七个数量级、字节相同输入返回不同排名且精确置换读出放大了该噪声。等待（0.805对0.800，五天复测）、切换供应商（四家供应商中位数0.74-0.88，与暴露的元数据无关）、自托管（仅在服务器空闲时有效）均无法达标。读出分离度由错误类型而非错误大小决定。

Conclusion: 在共享服务基础设施上，模型名不是冻结的测量仪器。预注册评估必须在冻结任何门槛之前先测量其仪器本身的可靠性。本文提供三层快照身份阶梯、八条设计规则和报告清单来指导实践；约2%研究调用量的试点即可提前暴露无法达标的门槛。

Abstract: Language-model judges now gate training data, score generations, and drive leaderboards. The judge is then a measurement instrument, resting on one rarely stated assumption: the same request, sent to the same model name, reads the same tomorrow. We audited that assumption in two preregistered campaigns with every threshold fixed in advance; neither got past validating its instrument. Across 52,988 audited request attempts, same-window repeat rankings agreed at Spearman 0.400 against a required 0.90, and byte-identical next-day replays agreed at 0.78 against a required 0.99, each time with the execution record at ceiling. Three mechanisms explain the gap: a label-to-meaning mapping that biased readouts as strongly as the signal; candidate gaps seven orders of magnitude below the instrument's own noise floor; and byte-identical inputs returning different rankings, a noise that exact-permutation readouts compound. Neither metric substitution nor sampling repaired it on the tested grid. Preregistered follow-ups bound the problem: waiting did not help on the days sampled (0.805 versus 0.800, replicated over five further days); switching providers did not help (four providers share the floor, medians 0.74 to 0.88, predicted by none of the metadata fields they expose); self-hosting on batch-invariant kernels helped only while the server was quiet; and on constructed errors with known gaps, the readout's separation tracks error type, not size. We distill the evidence into a three-level snapshot-identity ladder, eight design rules, and a reporting checklist; a pilot at roughly 2% of the study's call volume would have exposed both unreachable gates in advance. All results concern externally measured behaviour on shared serving infrastructure. On a shared endpoint, a model name is not a frozen instrument; a preregistered evaluation must measure its instrument before freezing any gate on it.

</details>

### [70] [Requirements After the First Edit: Mining Late Requirement Emergence and Rework in Real-World Coding-Agent Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03028)
*Bowen Jiang,Haowei Cheng,Yuhong Fu,Anne Koziolek,Jialong Li,Weixing Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文利用3,553个SWE-chat会话，量化了编码智能体场景中"后期需求浮现"（late requirement emergence）导致的代码失效问题：需求到达后约产生两倍于非需求编辑的代码替换/删除，且该负担在会话中不衰减，实验表明延迟披露会将实现转移到需求揭示之后。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利益相关者在系统部分存在之前往往无法表达约束，这一需求波动在传统项目中与进度和预算超支相关。然而现有编码智能体工作只部分弥合了这一差距：基准测试在实现前固定需求，观察性研究仅报告推回频率，未将需求到达与其引发的代码失效联系起来。

Method: 使用3,553个符合资格的SWE-chat会话，沿三个维度对实现后的需求到达进行编码；在可回放仓库状态的场景中，将每次需求到达与代理先前编写代码行的删除或替换这一代理指标关联。辅以受控实验，测试延迟披露与预先警告对代码覆盖的影响。

Result: 需求到达后引发的代码失效约为匹配的非需求编辑的两倍，该结果对用户轮次和净删除检查具有稳健性（但未证明因果关系）；此负担在会话中无显著下降趋势，且考虑多重性后与操作类型无显著关联；受控实验显示延迟披露会将实现转移到揭示之后，预先警告未检测到对覆盖的影响。

Conclusion: 后期需求浮现被确立为可测量的代码失效来源，为编码智能体中需求波动的影响提供了量化证据，并揭示了现有基准与观察研究在该问题上的测量空白。

Abstract: Coding agents often implement changes before users have fully articulated their requirements, echoing a pattern from requirements engineering: stakeholders cannot express a constraint until part of the system exists to react to. This volatility is associated with schedule and budget overruns in traditional projects, but only at release-cycle granularity. Existing work on coding agents narrows this gap only partway: curated benchmarks fix requirements before implementation by design, and observational studies report pushback frequency without linking arrivals to the code invalidation they cause. We address this using 3,553 eligible SWE-chat sessions, coding post-implementation requirement arrivals along three dimensions and, where repository state can be replayed, linking each arrival to a proxy: deletion or replacement of prior agent-authored lines. A requirement's arrival is followed by roughly twice as much invalidation as matched non-requirement edits, robust to user-turn and net-deletion checks, though not demonstrated as causal. This burden shows no detectable decline over a session and no detected association with operation type once multiplicity is accounted for; several intervals remain wide. A controlled experiment shows delayed disclosure relocates implementation post-reveal, while advance warning produces no detected effect on overwriting. These results establish late requirement emergence as a measurable source of code invalidation.

</details>


### [71] [Large Language Models and Language Server Protocol: a match made in context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03086)
*Alessandro Schena,Ilgiz Mustafin,Julia Kotovich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Eiffel-tools，一个针对Eiffel编程语言的LSP实现，结合大型语言模型（LLMs）和静态验证器来辅助开发经过形式化验证的软件。工具提供交互式和非交互式命令生成代码和规范，通过语言/项目特定知识指导LLM，并用静态验证器校验输出，支持重试直至验证通过。在2个公开数据集上用3个模型评估，bug修复率可达76%-95%，同时揭示了修复尝试次数与成功率之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型生成的代码通常缺乏形式化正确性保证，而静态验证软件（如Eiffel语言生态）对代码和规范的精确性要求极高。现有工具未能有效结合LLM的生成能力与形式化验证的可靠性，因此需要一种能自动生成并验证代码与规范的开发工具。

Method: 实现了一个语言服务器协议（LSP）工具，利用LLM生成代码和规范，通过三大策略提高输出的可靠性：(1) 使用语言和项目特定知识构建丰富的程序化提示词；(2) 使用静态验证器验证LLM输出，对错误结果进行纠正或拒绝；(3) 在验证失败时自动重试，直至程序通过形式化验证。

Result: 在2个公开数据集上使用3种不同模型进行评估，该工具结合LLM和形式化验证器可修复76%至95%的bug，具体成功率取决于所使用的模型和提示词策略。实验还揭示了修复尝试次数与成功率之间存在权衡关系。

Conclusion: Eiffel-tools成功展示了将LLM与形式化验证器相结合能为静态验证软件开发提供可靠辅助，既发挥了LLM的代码生成能力，又通过形式化验证保证了输出的正确性。修复尝试次数与成功率的权衡表明，用户需要在效率和成功率之间做出选择，这为后续在形式化验证软件开发中集成LLM提供了有价值的参考。

Abstract: This article introduces Eiffel-tools, a language server protocol (LSP) implementation for the Eiffel programming language that uses Large Language Models (LLMs) to aid the development of statically verified software. The tool provides various interactive and non-interactive commands to produce code and specifications. It uses language and project specific knowledge to precisely direct the LLM and verifies the output using a static verifier. It crafts rich programmatic prompts for the input and corrects or rejects the output. Furthermore, it handles the retries until the program passes verification. The tool's bug fixing capability is evaluated on 2 public datasets using 3 models. The tool can fix 76% to 95% of bugs by combining LLMs and a formal verifier depending on the model and prompts used. The results show the trade-off between the number of fixing attempts and the success rate.

</details>


### [72] [Compound Prompt Constraints in LLM Code Generation: A Factorial Study of Format, Persona, and Urgency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03156)
*Shrenik Jadhav,Nickalsa LaPlaca,Caleb Stone,Ashok Raja,Omar Ochoa,Vidhyashree Nagaraju*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过全因子实验设计（3x3x3共27种组合），系统研究了输出格式、角色设定和紧迫性框架如何联合影响LLM代码生成的可靠性。使用5个OpenAI模型（GPT-4o家族、GPT-4.1家族及o3-mini）对全部164个HumanEval+问题进行22,140次贪心解码评估，发现复合约束可产生架构依赖性的超加性退化效应，其中GPT-4o家族退化最明显（最大交互效应-12.2个百分点），GPT-4.1家族基本不受影响，而o3-mini反而表现改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境中的LLM提示词常常同时包含多个约束条件，而现有研究大多孤立地考察单一因素对代码生成质量的影响，忽略了多约束条件之间的交互效应。作者旨在揭示复合约束是否会产生单因素实验无法预测的联合退化现象，并为LLM辅助软件工程的可信性评估提供新的方法论框架。

Method: 采用受控3×3×3全因子设计，将输出格式化（JSON vs XML等）、角色指派（专家角色等）和紧迫性框架（三种水平）进行组合，共27种条件。将每个复合条件的表现分解为加性预测和残差交互项以捕捉超加性退化。对所有164个HumanEval+问题在5个模型上执行贪心解码（共22,140次评估），使用格式感知的提取管线区分格式失败与推理失败，并用McNemar检验、比值比和95%置信区间进行显著性评估。

Result: 复合约束能产生单因素实验无法预测的架构依赖退化。GPT-4o家族一致表现出超加性效应，pass@1相较加性预测额外下降3-12个百分点；最大交互效应为GPT-4o-mini在JSON+专家角色+中度紧迫性组合下的-12.2个百分点。JSON组合的交互效应通常大于XML。GPT-4.1家族基本不受影响，而o3-mini呈现不同模式——结构化输出约束反而能提升性能。

Conclusion: LLM对复合提示词的脆弱性具有架构依赖性而非规模依赖性；单独中性或有益的约束条件组合起来可能导致显著退化。因此，复合提示词测试应成为LLM辅助软件工程管线可靠性评估的标准组成部分。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in software engineering pipelines for code generation, where production prompts often combine multiple constraints. This paper presents a full-factorial empirical study of how output formatting, persona assignment, and urgency framing jointly affect LLM code-generation reliability. We evaluate all 27 combinations in a controlled 3x3x3 design and decompose each compound condition into an additive prediction and a residual interaction term that captures super-additive degradation. The study uses all 164 HumanEval+ problems across five OpenAI models from the GPT-4o family, GPT-4.1 family, and o3-mini, yielding 22,140 greedy-decoding evaluations. A format-aware extraction pipeline separates formatting failures from reasoning failures, and significance is assessed with McNemar's test, odds ratios, and 95% confidence intervals.
  Results show that compound constraints can produce architecture-dependent degradation not predictable from single-factor experiments. The GPT-4o family exhibits consistent super-additive effects, with pass@1 reductions 3-12 percentage points beyond additive predictions; the largest interaction is -12.2 pp on GPT-4o-mini for JSON + expert persona + moderate urgency. JSON combinations generally produce larger interactions than XML. In contrast, the GPT-4.1 family is largely resistant, while o3-mini shows a qualitatively different pattern in which structured output constraints can improve performance. These findings show that vulnerability is architecture-dependent rather than size-dependent, that individually neutral or beneficial constraints can combine to cause substantial degradation, and that compound-prompt testing should be standard in reliability assessment for LLM-assisted engineering pipelines.

</details>


### [73] [Two Truths and A Lie? Benchmarking Off-the-Shelf LLMs for Requirements Quality Assessment: Performance, False Alarms, and Misses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03230)
*Jannatul Shefa,Alejandro Salado,Paul Wach,Taylan G. Topcu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究首次对现成大语言模型（LLM）在需求工程质量评估中的表现进行基准测试，发现所有模型存在强烈的不对称错误分布——最佳模型也只能检测到专家识别的47%问题，且误报率11%。模型在需要系统工程判断的任务上（必要性、正确性）表现显著退化，代际进步非单调，采样温度影响有限。结论是现成LLM尚不可作为可信的自主评估者，其角色应限于人在回路的决策支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需求工程（RE）的质量决定系统工程（SE）下游所有环节的质量，有缺陷的需求会扩散为设计返工、进度延误和成本超支。由于需求常以自然语言撰写，生成式AI的进步使人们期望LLM能承担需求质量评估这一原本缓慢且高度依赖人工专家的工作。但关于LLM能否被信任从事此任务的实证证据仍然稀缺，亟需系统性的基准研究来验证。

Method: 基于INCOSE质量标准建立专家标注的真实基准，对OpenAI和Anthropic两个模型家族中的10个模型（覆盖两大家族、各五代）进行评估。实验包含100次独立运行、两组需求集和五种采样温度，系统性地测量各模型在需求质量问题检测中的性能指标。

Result: 四项关键发现：(1) 错误分布强烈不对称——最佳Anthropic模型仅检测到47%的专家识别问题，同时产生11%的误报；(2) 在需要系统工程判断的场景下性能显著退化，必要性和正确性问题几乎总是被遗漏；(3) 代际进步非单调，新模型不一定更好；(4) 错误行为在不同采样温度下仅发生轻微且非单调的变化，表明缺陷源自模型固有特性而非随机性。

Conclusion: 现成LLM尚不足以作为可信赖的自主需求质量评估者。对Agentic AI开发者而言，将这些LLM模块编排进专门架构中可能放大而非纠正其缺陷。其合理的近期角色是作为人在回路（human-in-the-loop）中的决策支持工具。

Abstract: Requirements engineering (RE) governs the quality of everything downstream in systems engineering (SE); defective requirements that survive review cycles propagate into design rework, schedule delays, and cost overruns. Because requirements are often written in natural language, recent advances in generative AI have raised expectations that large language models (LLMs) can absorb requirement quality assessment, a task otherwise slow and human expertise-intensive. Yet empirical evidence on whether LLMs can be trusted to do so remains scarce. This study presents the first benchmarking analysis of off-the-shelf LLM performance for requirement quality evaluation. Against an expert-derived ground truth built on INCOSE quality criteria, we evaluate ten models spanning two families (OpenAI and Anthropic) and five generations each, across one hundred independent runs, two requirement sets, and five sampling temperatures. Four contributions follow. First, we quantify a strongly asymmetric error profile: across all models and runs, the best-performing Anthropic model detects a median of only 47% of expert-identified issues while false-flagging 11%. Second, performance degrades significantly where SE judgment is required, as necessity and correctness issues are almost always missed. Third, generational progress is non-monotonic, so newer models cannot be assumed better. Fourth, this error behavior shifts only modestly and non-monotonically across sampling temperatures, indicating characteristic model deficiencies rather than inherent stochasticity. Off-the-shelf LLMs are therefore not yet trustworthy autonomous evaluators. Findings also warrant caution for Agentic AI developers: orchestrating these LLM modules in specialized architectures risks compounding these deficiencies rather than correcting them. Their defensible near-term role is human-in-the-loop decision support.

</details>


### [74] [Refusing the Impossible: A Taxonomy and Benchmark for Code Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03267)
*Vishnu Asutosh Dasu,Ashish Kundu,Gang Tan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了大型语言模型（LLM）的"代码幻觉"问题，将其定义为"无根基生成"并与普通"代码错误"区分，提出了三维分类法（根基性、表现层面、行为），构建了由270个对抗性不可满足提示和91个可解对照组成的基准套件，评估了12个开源模型，发现模型在约60%的不可满足提示上生成无根基代码，仅27%正确拒绝。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM越来越多地被用于代码生成，但其产出经常看似合理却不基于现实事实——例如导入不存在的包，或声称实现了违反已验证定理的算法，且仍然能编译和运行。此前缺乏对这一独特问题（代码幻觉）的系统性定义、分类和量化评估，需要将其与普通代码错误区分开来，以推动针对性的缓解策略。

Method: 作者提出：(1) 一个三维分类学——根基性（绝对违反普遍真理 vs. 相对捏造情境或生态系统特定事实）、表现层面（语法、语义或事实）和行为（从信心满满的捏造到退化输出），并按严重程度排序；(2) 构建一个对抗性基准套件，包含刻意不可满足的任务（正确响应是拒绝），涵盖6种语言、24个子类别共270个提示，配套91个可匹配的可解对照；(3) 采用两级人工标注协议，与人类标签验证（82%一致性，κ=0.73），对12个开源权重代码和推理模型共4,332个评判响应进行分析。

Result: 评估发现：模型在约60%的不可满足提示上生成无根基代码，仅27%的提示被正确拒绝；但在可解对照上错误拒绝率仅为0%，说明模型的失败模式是过度自信地生成虚假代码而非过度保守地拒绝。

Conclusion: LLMs在代码生成中面临严重的代码幻觉问题——特别是面对不可能/不可满足的任务时，模型往往编造看似合理但不真实的内容而不是承认无法完成。这一系统性量化和分类为未来提高代码生成可靠性和开发幻觉减轻技术奠定了基准基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often produce code that looks plausible but is not grounded in reality. The code may import packages that do not exist or claim to implement algorithms that violate proven theorems, while still compiling and running. We study \emph{code hallucination} as \emph{ungrounded generation} and separate it from ordinary \emph{code error} (bugs in otherwise grounded programs). We propose a taxonomy with three dimensions: \textbf{groundedness} (absolute violations of universal truths vs.\ relative fabrications of contingent or ecosystem-specific facts), \textbf{manifestation level} (syntactic, semantic, or factual), and \textbf{behavior} (from confident fabrication to degenerate output), organized into a severity ordering. We build an \textbf{adversarial} suite of deliberately unsatisfiable tasks where the correct response is to refuse and categorize the responses under our taxonomy. The suite contains \textbf{270 prompts} across six languages and 24 subcategories, paired with \textbf{91 matched solvable controls}, and responses are judged by a two-tier protocol validated against human labels (82\% agreement, $κ{=}0.73$). Across twelve open-weight code and reasoning models (4{,}332 judged responses), models produce ungrounded code on about 60\% of unsatisfiable prompts and refuse only 27\%, while wrongly refusing 0\% of the solvable controls.

</details>


### [75] [TIPCODER: Reinforcement Learning Boosted Test-time Instruction Proposer for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03309)
*Minyu Chen,Sihao Wu,Ling-I Wu,Song Qin,Jingyang Li,Lei Ning,Jianxin Xue,Guoqiang Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TipCoder提出了一种实例级别的指令空间探索方法，在代码生成时先为问题生成辅助提示，再基于这些提示生成解决方案，并通过奖励模型进行选择，从而在测试时间实现指令层面的缩放，优于随机采样和通用提示优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的测试时间缩放通过在固定指令下采样多个程序来探索解空间，但许多代码生成失败源于原始提示中的约束缺失、边界情况遗漏或误导性推理路径。作者希望从互补的指令空间维度进行探索，以解决这类由指令本身引起的问题。

Method: TipCoder包含三个关键组件：首先将多轮调试轨迹蒸馏为主动指导（tips）；其次使用边际效用奖励通过强化学习优化Proposer；推理时同时生成基础方案和tip引导方案，并利用奖励模型进行事后选择。

Result: 在多个代码生成基准和目标代码LLM上，TipCoder提供了一致的指令级测试时间缩放策略，在共享奖励模型选择协议下优于随机采样和通用提示优化基线。

Conclusion: 这种探索-选择设计使得tips能够暴露额外的候选潜力，同时减少不必要的指导带来的回归，验证了指令空间探索作为代码生成测试时间缩放的有效互补方向。

Abstract: Test-time scaling for code generation typically explores the solution space by sampling multiple programs from a fixed instruction. We study a complementary direction: instance-level instruction-space exploration. Our observation is that many coding failures stem from missing constraints, overlooked edge cases, or misleading reasoning paths induced by the original prompt. To address this, we propose TipCoder, a test-time instruction proposer that generates problem-specific auxiliary tips before code synthesis. TipCoder distills multi-turn debugging trajectories into proactive guidance and further optimizes the Proposer with reinforcement learning using a marginal-utility reward. At inference time, it generates both a base solution and a tip-guided solution, and applies a Reward Model for post-hoc selection. This exploration-selection design allows tips to expose additional candidate potential while reducing regressions from unnecessary guidance. Across the evaluated code-generation benchmarks and target Code LLMs, TipCoder provides a consistent instruction-level test-time scaling strategy, comparing favorably with stochastic sampling and generic prompt optimization baselines under a shared reward-model-based selection protocol.

</details>


### [76] [The Psychological Costs of Artificial Intelligence Adoption in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03456)
*Adam Alami,Elda Paja,Abhishek Tiwari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过在一家大型软件开发服务公司的案例研究（一年后评估AI采用情况，21次半结构化访谈），揭示了软件专业人员在组织采用AI过程中经历的心理成本，包括责任焦虑、工艺身份断裂、意义与满意度侵蚀、认知和工作负荷加剧以及不确定性困扰，并识别了从业者应对这些成本的三种方式（恢复控制、保护性适应和吸收）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管组织积极在软件工程中引入AI以提升如生产力等有形产出，但AI作为一种破坏性力量被引入到角色身份、团队规范和职业满意度此前已建立稳定的工作环境中。历史经验表明技术颠覆会给职场带来心理和社会压力（如焦虑、技能退化、职业身份受损），因此假定AI用于软件工程是零成本的并不准确，作者旨在理解软件专业人员在组织AI采纳过程中经历的心理成本。

Method: 采用案例研究方法，在一家大型软件开发服务公司发布AI采用策略一年后进行实地研究。通过会议和半结构化访谈（N=21）收集定性数据，并使用主题分析等方法进行归纳分析。

Result: 发现软件专业人员经历五类心理成本：责任焦虑、工艺身份断裂、意义与满意度侵蚀、认知与工作量加剧、不确定性困扰。从业者通过恢复控制的实践、保护性和身份保全性适应、以及吸收无法解决的成本三种方式来管理和应对这些心理成本。

Conclusion: 将软件工程中AI与人类协作的AI采用重新定位为一场人类转型（human transition），而非仅仅是技术和组织层面的转型。研究强调应关注AI采用过程中心理和社会层面的影响，而非仅关注生产力等有形成果。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is increasingly used to augment software engineering (SE) workflows. While code generation remains the main use case, organizations are actively seeking AI integration in other practices such as test cases generation and code reviews. Organizational AI adoption strategies seem to focus on tangible outcomes such as productivity. However, AI is a disruptive force, introduced into settings where role identity, team norms, and the sources of job satisfaction were well established before the recent advances in generative AI. Historically, technological disruptions have caused psychological and social strains in workplaces, ranging from anxiety and eroded meaning to deskilling and disrupted professional identities. The assumption that AI for SE is cost-free may not be accurate. Therefore, in this study we sought to understand the psychological costs software professionals experience during organizational AI adoption. We carried out a case study in a large software development services company, one year after the company launched its AI adoption. We collected qualitative data through meetings and semi-structured interviews (N = 21). We found that software professionals experience accountability anxiety, craft identity disruption, meaning and satisfaction erosion, cognitive and workload intensification, and uncertainty distress. Practitioners manage these costs through practices that restore control, mitigate them through protective and identity-preserving adaptations, or absorb them, carrying what neither can resolve. We contribute to AI-human collaboration in SE by repositioning AI adoption as a human transition, not only a technological and organizational one.

</details>


### [77] [Code Transformation Rule Synthesis using LLMs: Potential and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03592)
*Axel Allain,Aymeric Blot,Djamel Eddine Khelladi,Mathieu Acher*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地实证研究三个领域特定语言（Comby、GritQL、Ast-Grep）中，三个LLM（GPT-5.4、GPT-oss-120B、Llama3.1-8B）在四个软件演化任务（API误用修正、程序修复、API迁移、语言版本迁移）上的变换规则合成能力，发现GPT-5.4在规则适用性和正确性上表现优异，小模型在简单场景下可用但复杂迁移任务中力不从心，且LLMs在正确性上优于anti-unification算法但适用性略逊。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM存在黑盒性质、缺乏可解释性和确定性，且在大代码库上的重复任务使用成本高。将LLM将结构化代码变换任务转化为可复用的领域特定语言（DSL）规则，可以降低推理成本、增强确定性和可解释性。但此前该方向缺乏系统性的实证评估。

Method: 本研究设计了一个覆盖六个多样化数据集的实证框架，评估三个LLM（GPT-5.4、GPT-oss-120B、Llama3.1-8B）在Comby、GritQL和Ast-Grep三种DSL中的规则合成能力。任务涵盖四类软件演化场景：API误用修正、程序修复、API迁移和语言版本迁移。通过规则适用率、与ground truth的接近度、元变量使用和复用得分等指标进行多维评估，并与anti-unification基线算法对比。

Result: GPT-5.4在大多数基准上实现持续高规则适用率，生成最接近ground truth的变换。GPT-oss-120B和Llama3.1-8B在简单局部变更上有效，但在复杂迁移场景中表现不足。模型展现出通过元变量实现的可泛化性和高复用得分。与anti-unification相比，LLM在正确性上表现更好，但规则适用性略低。

Conclusion: 变换规则合成已超越概念验证阶段，强前沿模型（如GPT-5.4）已能生成稳健、正确、可泛化且可复用的DSL规则。小模型在特定简单场景下仍有一定实用价值，但整体表明LLM在结构化规则生成上展示了巨大潜力。

Abstract: Due to their black-box nature, LLMs suffer from limited explain- ability and a lack of determinism. Their usage cost can also rise, particularly with repetitive tasks on large codebases. To mitigate this, we conduct a novel empirical study targeting three domain- specific languages for transformation rules, namely Comby, GritQL, and Ast-Grep. We evaluate three LLMs (GPT-5.4, GPT-oss-120B, and Llama3.1-8B) on six diverse datasets covering four software- evolution tasks: API misuse correction, program repair, API migra- tion, and language version migration. Our results provide evidence that transformation rule synthesis moves beyond proof-of-concept with strong frontier models. GPT-5.4 achieves consistently high rule applicability rates and produces transformations closest to the ground truth across most benchmarks. Smaller and open-weight GPT-oss-120B and Llama3.1-8B models remain effective for simpler, localized changes but struggle with complex migration scenarios. We also observe non-negligible generalizability through the usage of meta-variables and through a high reuse score in the first quartile of many datasets. Finally, when compared to the anti-unification algorithm, LLMs outperform it in correctness, but underperform in rule applicability. Overall, our results show great potential for LLMs to generate sound, correct, generalizable, and reusable rules.

</details>


### [78] [Can LLMs Extract Architectural Design Decisions from Source Code Commits? - A Preliminary Exploratory Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03721)
*Amey Karan,Rudra Dhar,Mohamed Soliman,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文针对从源代码提交中恢复架构设计决策（ADD）这一任务，初步评估了四种大语言模型（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3）在零样本和少样本提示下的表现。实验表明所有模型的BERT-F1均超过0.81，少样本提示能提升效果，但生成的ADD往往过长且偏向实现细节，缺乏对决策背后动机的捕捉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构设计决策（ADD）记录软件系统结构与演化的背后动机，但很少被显式文档化，通常隐藏在源代码提交中。恢复这些决策对架构知识管理（AKM）至关重要，然而从提交中提取ADD因其实隐式和非结构化特点而极具挑战。大语言模型在理解代码和文本方面展现出强大能力，但其在此任务中的有效性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 作者使用四种LLM（Gemini 3 Pro、DeepSeek R1、Kimi K2、Qwen3），采用零样本和少样本提示策略，在30条来自开源项目的开发者撰写的ADD上进行实验。使用ROUGE-L、BLEU、METEOR和BERTScore进行自动评分，另由一位作者对Gemini输出进行人工审查。

Result: 所有模型的BERT-F1均高于0.81；少样本提示能改善对齐效果（Gemini的BERT-F1从0.828提升至0.847）。然而，生成的ADD普遍过长、过于偏向实现细节，且遗漏了决策背后的设计动机。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在ADD恢复任务上已展现一定的能力，但在架构感知方面仍有明显不足，未来需要开发更具架构感知能力的LLM系统以支持自动化架构知识管理。

Abstract: Context: Architectural Design Decisions (ADDs) capture the rationale behind the structure and evolution of software systems but are rarely documented explicitly, and are often hidden inside source code commits. Recovering them is important for Architectural Knowledge Management (AKM). Problem: Extracting ADDs from commits is challenging due to their implicit and unstructured nature. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in understanding code and text, yet their effectiveness for this task remains underexplored. Study: We present a preliminary study using four LLMs (Gemini 3 Pro, DeepSeek R1, Kimi K2, Qwen3) with zeroshot and fewshot prompting on 30 developer-written ADDs from open-source projects. We score outputs with ROUGE-L, BLEU, METEOR, and BERTScore, and one author manually reviews the Gemini outputs. Results: All models reach a BERT-F1 above 0.81, and fewshot prompting improves alignment (Gemini BERT-F1: 0.828 to 0.847). However, the generated ADDs are often too long, implementation-focused, and miss the rationale behind the decision. This highlights opportunities for architecture-aware LLM systems and automated AKM.

</details>


### [79] [No One Left Behind: Cross-Level Analysis for Sustainable Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03861)
*Masoum Salehi,Sandro Schulze,Jacob Krüger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"可持续性反模式"（sustainability antipatterns）概念，用于捕获软件工程中系统性地导致不可持续结果的条件，帮助揭示跨社会技术层面与可持续性维度之间的错位问题，并为可持续软件工程研究提供整合性研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件可持续性研究（如能源效率、资源优化、代码可维护性）多集中于单一维度或特定工件，缺乏对可持续性问题如何在软件工程的社会技术层面（组织、流程、产品）涌现和相互强化的解释。作者认为可持续性挑战本质上是系统性的，源于组织优先级、开发实践与技术条件之间的交互作用。

Method: 本文为一篇愿景论文（vision paper），提出"可持续性反模式"这一新概念框架，用于系统性地刻画导致不可持续结果的条件，并据此展示如何暴露跨层面的错配问题；同时为未来的识别、形式化、检测和缓解可持续性反模式提供具体研究方向。

Result: 文章论证了可持续性反模式能够捕捉在工件中心或维度特定分析中难以识别的跨层错配，并提出可持续性反模式可作为可持续软件工程领域的整合性、可操作研究议程的基础。

Conclusion: 可持续性反模式有望成为可持续软件工程研究的基础性概念工具，推动从单维度、静态分析走向系统性、跨层面的诊断与干预，为未来研究提供明确方向。

Abstract: Software engineers expect a software system to be efficient, maintainable, economically viable, and socially responsible throughout its lifecycle. Unfortunately, decisions made at the organizational, process, or product levels of software development - even when they improve one of these goals - often have unintended long-term consequences at other levels. In response, software-engineering research has explored different ways to achieve software sustainability, including energy efficiency, resource optimization, and code maintainability. However, existing research offers limited explanations for how sustainability problems emerge and reinforce one another across the socio-technical levels of software engineering. Moreover, we argue that sustainability challenges are not isolated but rather systemic: They stem from interactions among organizational priorities, development practices, and technical conditions. In this vision paper, we introduce the concept of sustainability antipatterns to capture conditions that systematically produce unsustainable outcomes. We demonstrate how this concept can help expose cross-level misalignments between socio-technical levels and sustainability dimensions that remain difficult to recognize through artifact-centric or dimension-specific analyses. To guide future research, we outline concrete directions for identifying, formalizing, detecting, and mitigating sustainability anti-patterns. We envision sustainability anti-patterns as a foundation for an integrated and actionable research agenda on sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [80] [LabelMate: An LLM-Driven Framework for Refined Issue Report Labeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04055)
*Liam Johnston,Shayan Noei,Maram Assi,Ying Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LabelMate是一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的框架，能够从历史issue报告中自动推导出项目特定的标签体系，并为新issue自动分配标签，无需任何预标注训练数据。在16,500条来自30个GitHub仓库的issue报告上评估，生成了275个连贯标签，平均标注准确率达89.84%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件issue报告的标注对于项目规划和社区参与至关重要，但许多报告因手工设计标签分类法和分配标签的巨额工作量而未被标注。现有自动化方法存在以下关键局限：需要大量人工干预、只能分配通用标签、依赖已有标注数据集。

Method: LabelMate框架包含两个核心步骤：(1) 从历史issue报告中自动推导一个全面且项目特定的标签集合；(2) 自动为新的issue报告分配相关标签。整个过程完全由LLM驱动，不需要任何预标注的训练数据。

Result: 在30个多样化的GitHub仓库的16,500条issue报告上评估，LabelMate生成了275个标签的连贯列表，平均标注准确率达到89.84%，与现有通用标签分配方法相比有统计学上的显著提升。

Conclusion: LabelMate提供了一种高效、领域自适应的解决方案来简化issue标注流程，克服了现有方法对人工干预、通用标签和标注数据集依赖的问题。

Abstract: Software users often submit issue reports to a product's issue tracking system to report defects, suggest enhancements, or raise other product-related concerns. Labeling these issue reports supports effective planning and improves community engagement. However, many issue reports remain unlabeled due to the substantial manual effort required to design an appropriate label taxonomy, then assign suitable labels from this taxonomy to new issue reports. Existing automated labeling approaches attempt to mitigate these challenges. However, they suffer from key limitations, such as extensive manual intervention, the assignment of generic labels, and a dependence on existing labeled datasets. To address these limitations, we propose LabelMate, a novel Large Language Model (LLM)-driven framework that (1) derives a comprehensive, project-specific label set from historical issue reports and (2) automatically assigns relevant labels to new issue reports without requiring any pre-labeled training data. We evaluate LabelMate on 16,500 issue reports from 30 popular and diverse GitHub repositories. Based on this dataset, our approach generates a coherent list of 275 labels and achieves an average labeling accuracy of 89.84%, a statistically significant improvement over existing generic label assigning approaches. These results demonstrate that LabelMate offers an efficient, domain-adaptive solution to streamline the issue labeling process.

</details>


### [81] [When Models Edit Too Much: On the Fidelity of Minimal Code Edits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04061)
*Tongyao Zhu,Wei Hern Lim,Min-Yen Kan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型在代码修复中的"过度编辑"问题——模型倾向于重写超出修复所需的代码。作者通过在400个BigCodeBench问题中注入AST级别的受控破坏构建评测框架，发现前沿模型普遍存在过度编辑现象。通过"保留指令"可显著减少该行为（平均超额编辑距离从0.195降至0.131，认知复杂度降低26.6%，Pass@1提升2.3个点）；在微调实验中，强化学习相比监督微调在域外编辑保真度和性能保持上达到更优平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在代码编辑中仅追求正确性是不够的，有用的修复还应最小化、可审查且忠实于原始实现。现有研究缺乏对"过度编辑"这一质量维度的系统度量与分析，因此作者希望构建可量化的评测框架并探索如何控制和学习"编辑保真度"。

Method: 从400个BigCodeBench问题中选取参考解法，注入AST级别的受控损坏（确保每个修复任务有已知的最小补丁），构建评测框架。测试多个前沿LLM（包括GPT-5.5等），对比Pass@1、平均超额Levenshtein距离和新增认知复杂度等指标。随后实验两种干预方式：(1) 在提示中加入"保留指令"；(2) 对比监督微调与强化学习在编辑保真度上的后训练效果，评估域内过拟合与域外泛化表现。

Result: 过度编辑在前沿LLM中广泛存在，即便强模型如GPT-5.5也出现高Pass@1与不必要的大幅编辑并存的情况。"保留指令"显著降低过度编辑行为（超额Levenshtein距离从0.195降至0.131，认知复杂度降低26.6%，Pass@1提升2.3点），但此增益不能单纯归因于更大推理预算或更大模型。后训练实验中，监督微调过拟合于已见的损坏模式，而强化学习在域外编辑保真度和性能保持之间取得最佳权衡。

Conclusion: 编辑保真度（edit fidelity）是代码修复质量中独立于正确性的一个重要维度，可以被系统度量，也能够在后训练阶段被学习。该研究为LLM代码修复的质量评估和训练策略提供了新的视角和方法论基础。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to edit existing code, but correctness alone is not enough: useful repairs should also be minimal, reviewable, and faithful to the original implementation. We study over-editing, the tendency of a model to rewrite code beyond what is required to fix a bug. We construct an evaluation framework from 400 BigCodeBench problems by injecting controlled AST-level corruptions into reference solutions, giving each repair task a known minimal patch. Across frontier LLMs, over-editing is widespread even among strong models like GPT-5.5: high Pass@1 can coexist with unnecessarily large edits and added cognitive complexity. A preservation instruction substantially reduces this behavior, lowering average excess Levenshtein distance from 0.195 to 0.131, reducing added cognitive complexity by 26.6%, and increasing Pass@1 by 2.3 points. However, these gains do not simply follow from a larger reasoning budget or larger models. We next ask whether minimal editing can be learned directly during post-training. We observe that supervised fine-tuning overfits to seen corruption patterns, whereas reinforcement learning gives the best out-of-domain edit-fidelity and performance-retention trade-off. These results position edit fidelity as a distinct axis of code-repair quality and show that it can be measured and learned.

</details>


### [82] [ATIBA: Grounded Integrity and Quality Checking for Research Papers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04123)
*Veli Karakaya,Semih Çağlar,Yusuf Yiğit Korkmaz,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ATIBA是一个基于LLM的学术稿件完整性检查工具，提供五项基于证据锚定的质量检查（参考文献完整性、投稿合规性、实证标准合规性、AI审稿和引文建议），其核心原则是LLM只被信任用于判断、不被信任去凭空创造证据。通过13位非作者的调节用户研究，六项调查题目的同意率在69%-92%之间（均值85%），初步验证了感知有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手稿的参考文献完整性检查、对目标会议特定投稿规则的合规检查、以及对社区报告标准的遵守检查，目前都是人工、重复且因会议而异的工作，在实践中往往执行不一致甚至被跳过，迫切需要自动化工具来系统化这些流程。

Method: ATIBA包含五项基于grounding原则的检查：（1）参考文献完整性检查——将每条引文与文献来源核对并标记撤稿或无法找到的引用；（2）会议/方向合规检查——直接从会议的call-for-papers页面提取投稿标准并逐一评估稿件，每个判定锚定到页面的原文引用；（3）实证标准合规检查——对照ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards，并设有幻觉防御机制，丢弃任何无法在稿件中找到原文的证据引用；（4）基于GPT-5.4（通过Azure OpenAI）的多模式AI审稿（会议特定、正式和页面锚定的注释）；（5）引文建议功能——为稿件推荐候选参考文献并在展示给用户前逐条验证。所有检查共享同一设计原则：LLM仅被授权判断，不得捏造其判断所依据的证据。评估采用13位非作者的调节用户研究。

Result: 在13位非作者参与者的调节用户研究中，六项调查题目的同意率范围为69%-92%，均值为85%，为各评估工作流的积极感知有用性提供了初步证据。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了ATIBA在用户感知层面的有用性；然而客观的准确性指标仍有待未来测量验证。

Abstract: Checking a manuscript's reference integrity, its compliance with a target venue's specific submission rules, and its adherence to community reporting standards is manual, repetitive, and different for every venue so in practice it is done inconsistently or skipped. We present ATIBA, a tool that runs five grounded integrity and quality checks on a manuscript: a reference-integrity check that verifies each citation against bibliographic sources and flags retracted or unfindable references; a venue/track compliance check that derives submission criteria directly from a venue's own call-for-papers page and evaluates the manuscript against them, each verdict anchored to a verbatim quote from that page; an empirical-standards compliance check against the ACM SIGSOFT Empirical Standards, with a hallucination defence that discards any evidence quote it cannot locate verbatim in the manuscript; a multi-mode AI review (venue-specific, formal, and page-anchored annotation) powered by GPT-5.4 through Azure OpenAI; and a citation-suggestion feature that proposes candidate references for a manuscript and verifies each against bibliographic sources before it is shown to the user. All five checks are designed around the same principle: an LLM is only trusted to judge, never to invent the evidence it judges against. We evaluated ATIBA through a moderated user study with 13 non-author participants. Agreement across the six survey items ranged from 69% to 92%, with a mean of 85%, providing initial evidence of positive perceived usefulness across the evaluated workflows. These findings establish perceived usefulness; objective accuracy remains to be measured.

</details>


### [83] [SWE-Gate: Passing Functional Tests Is Not Enough for Software Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04167)
*Xin He,Yanlin Wang,Mingwei Liu,Jiachi Chen,Hongyu Zhang,Guanbin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-Gate是一个仓库级别的软件工程智能体基准测试，在功能正确性之外显式评估审查约束（review constraints）的合规性。它从真实PR审查评论中提取约束并构建303个修复实例，实验发现644个通过功能测试的修复中有221个未满足审查约束，表明仅基于功能的评估高估了智能体满足完整修复需求的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级软件工程基准主要衡量生成的补丁是否通过功能测试，忽略了实际软件开发中影响补丁是否被接受的审查派生约束（如代码风格、架构规范、边界处理等），导致对智能体真实能力的评估不全面。

Method: 1) 从真实PR审查评论中提取审查约束；2) 围绕这些约束合成仓库级修复实例；3) 每个实例分别提供功能性测试和约束性测试，以及不合规补丁和黄金补丁，从而显式区分问题解决能力和审查约束合规性；4) 使用统一的编码智能体脚手架，在四种不同能力水平的LLM后端上进行实验评估。

Result: 构建了包含303个仓库级修复实例的SWE-Gate基准，覆盖75个开源Python仓库。在644个通过功能测试的修复中，有221个未能满足提供的审查约束，揭示了功能成功与完整修复规范成功之间存在显著差距。

Conclusion: 仅功能性的评估会高估智能体满足仓库级修复任务完整需求的能力，审查约束合规性应作为软件工程智能体评估中的独立关键维度。代码、数据和实验结果已开源提供。

Abstract: Repository-level software engineering benchmarks have significantly advanced the evaluation of coding agents, but existing benchmarks primarily measure whether generated patches pass functional tests and overlook review-derived acceptance constraints (review constraints) that often influence whether a patch is acceptable in real-world software development. We introduce SWE-Gate, a repository-level benchmark for software engineering agents that explicitly evaluates review constraint compliance alongside functional correctness.
