SWE-Gate derives review constraints from real pull request review comments and synthesizes repository-level repair instances around these constraints. Each instance provides separate functional and constraint tests, together with non-compliant and gold patches, enabling explicit separation between issue resolution capability and review constraint compliance. We construct SWE-Gate with 303 repository-level repair instances spanning 75 open-source Python repositories across diverse software domains. Experiments with four LLM backends spanning different capability levels under a common coding-agent scaffold reveal a substantial gap between functional success and success under the complete repair specification: among 644 repairs that pass the functional tests, 221 fail to satisfy the provided review constraints. These findings show that functional-only evaluation overestimates agents' ability to satisfy the full requirements of repository-level repair tasks. The replication package including code, data, and experimental results is available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/SWE-Gate.

</details>


### [84] [ExecRetrieval: Measuring the Functional-Correctness Gap in Code-Embedding Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01865)
*Aaryan Kapoor,Md Abdullah Al Hafiz Khan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ExecRetrieval基准，包含939个Python任务，每个任务配有执行验证的正确实现和最多4个"近克隆但有缺陷"的干扰项（均由单次编辑的机械突变生成），用于测试代码检索嵌入能否在功能层面区分正确与近似但不正确的代码。评测23种密集嵌入配置和BM25后发现，即使排名前1命中率较高，主流系统在67-78%查询中将正确代码排名低于至少一个配对缺陷变体，揭示了嵌入在功能鉴别上的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码检索基准的搜索池中缺乏受控的、经执行验证的单一编辑变异体作为每个查询规范实现的反事实干扰项，因此无法回答"嵌入能否在检索场景中功能性地区分正确代码与近乎克隆但不正确的代码"这一问题。解决此问题需要一个搜索池本身包含相关反事实的基准。

Method: 构建ExecRetrieval基准，包含939个Python任务（从MBPP和HumanEval改编而来），每个任务配有一个经执行验证的正确实现和最多4个经执行验证的有缺陷干扰项，每个干扰项由单一定向编辑的机械突变生成。在提供商原生调用方式下评估23种密集嵌入配置及BM25，使用配对McNemar检验和查询级bootstrap区间进行统计验证。

Result: 将近克隆反事实加入搜索池后，表现最佳的主机系统exec@10=1.00但exec@1仅0.331；排名第1的失误中配对缺陷变体占91.5-99.4%（四个领先系统）；在67-78%的查询中，领先系统将正确实现排名低于其至少一个配对缺陷干扰项。揭示了即便最强嵌入系统在功能判别任务上的严重不足。

Conclusion: 当前领先的代码检索嵌入系统无法可靠地进行功能层面的代码鉴别——当正确代码与其经执行验证的缺陷变体高度相似时，检索器在多数查询中无法将正确的排名前置。人工构建的语义或词汇指纹优于标注嵌入而未获显著提升的配置，表明嵌入在检测精确执行语义偏差方面存在系统性局限。数据集、执行预言机、嵌入矩阵与统计检验均已发布。

Abstract: Embedding-based code retrieval is a core component of coding agents and retrieval-augmented code generation, where retrieving correct code matters more than retrieving lexically similar code. Existing code-retrieval benchmarks do not plant controlled, execution-verified single-edit variants of each query's canonical implementation in the search pool, leaving the question of whether embeddings can functionally discriminate correct from near-clone-but-incorrect code unanswered in a retrieval setting. Resolving this requires a benchmark whose search pool itself contains the relevant counterfactuals -- execution-verified buggy variants near-identical to each canonical -- so that a retriever's rank ordering can be directly tested for functional discrimination rather than topical or identity overlap. We introduce ExecRetrieval, 939 Python tasks each paired with one execution-verified canonical implementation and up to four execution-verified buggy distractors, each generated by a mechanical mutation making a single targeted edit, and evaluate 23 dense embedding configurations plus BM25 under provider-native invocation with paired McNemar tests and query-level bootstrap intervals. With near-clone counterfactuals in the pool, the top hosted system reaches exec@10 = 1.00 but only exec@1 = 0.331; rank-1 misses are paired buggy variants 91.5-99.4% of the time across the four leading systems, and the canonical scores below at least one of its four paired distractors in 67-78% of queries on the leading systems. The full dataset, execution oracle, embedding matrices, environment snapshot, and pairwise statistical tests are released at the URL in Appendix D.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [CHSR-RRF: A curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework with reciprocal rank fusion and leakage-aware benchmarking for educational RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02913)
*Terence Ateya,Zavier Ndum Ndum,Jicheng Fu,Kelly Tendongkeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CHSR-RRF框架（基于课程门控的混合检索方法，在检索前施加元数据约束，结合稀疏/密集检索与倒数排名融合）及CERB基准（126个案例的课程约束检索基准），解决教育问答中"课程泄漏"问题——即检索结果虽主题相关但不符合学科、年级或考试情境的失效模式。61案例试点显示检索前门控将泄漏降低4.6倍且保持排名召回率，而检索后施加约束则导致召回归零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG检索器仅优化主题相关性，未强制执行课程有效性约束。在学校场景中，检索到的段落可能来自错误的学科、年级或考试情境，即使主题相关也不适合使用，这种失效模式被称为"课程泄漏"。作者认为教育领域检索应被视为受约束的选择问题。

Method: 提出CHSR-RRF框架，核心思想是在检索之前（pre-retrieval）对文档施加元数据门控约束，再结合稀疏检索（BM25）和稠密检索（向量检索）并通过倒数排名融合（RRF）及确定性重排序。同时构建CERB基准，包含126个案例，具有层级感知的相关性标签和显式泄漏标注，用于评估课程约束检索效果。

Result: 在61案例试点中，检索前门控将课程泄漏减少4.6倍（p<0.001）且保持排名召回率；对比之下，在检索后施加相同约束会导致召回率和精确范围成功率完全崩塌至零（p=0.039）。全基准下界分析显示，剩余的许多失败源于语料库和元数据本身的缺口，而非单纯的检索设计问题。

Conclusion: 在结构化教育领域中，检索应被视为受约束的选择过程，有效性约束应在候选池形成阶段（即检索前）强制执行，而非在排名之后再施加。这一结论对RAG在教育场景中的应用设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is increasingly used in educational question answering, but standard retrievers optimize topical relevance without enforcing curriculum validity. In school settings, a passage can be relevant yet inappropriate if it comes from the wrong subject, level, or examination context; we call this failure mode curriculum leakage. We present CHSR-RRF, a curriculum-gated hybrid retrieval framework that applies metadata constraints before retrieval, then combines sparse and dense search with reciprocal rank fusion and deterministic reranking. We also introduce CERB, a 126-case benchmark for curriculum-constrained retrieval with hierarchy-aware relevance labels and explicit leakage annotations. On a 61-case pilot, pre-retrieval gating reduces leakage by 4.6x ($p<0.001$) while preserving ranked recall, whereas applying the same constraints after retrieval collapses recall and exact-scope success to zero ($p=0.039$). A full-benchmark lower-bound analysis further shows that many remaining failures arise from corpus and metadata gaps rather than retrieval design alone. These results show that retrieval in structured educational domains should be treated as constrained selection, with validity enforced when the candidate pool is formed rather than after ranking.

</details>


### [86] [Reflect-SQL: A Self-Reflection Based Framework for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02944)
*Anupreksha Jain,Manish Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Reflect-SQL，一个基于多阶段自我反思的Text-to-SQL框架，通过知识库理解模糊数据库模式、反馈驱动的检索循环、以及LLM-as-a-judge驱动的SQL合成与验证循环，在BIRD基准上达到72.03%的执行准确率，显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业实际部署Text-to-SQL面临三大挑战：晦涩且规模庞大的数据库模式难以理解；因模式结构化和用户查询模糊导致相关表/列检索低效；缺乏稳健的验证与纠错机制导致生成语法或逻辑错误的SQL。现有单次生成方法无法系统性解决上述问题。

Method: Reflect-SQL采用多阶段自我反思架构：(1) 利用知识库增强对模糊模式的理解；(2) 反馈驱动的检索循环迭代精化用户自然语言查询；(3) LLM-as-a-judge驱动的综合循环对SQL进行验证与修正；(4) 蕴含循环优化端到端流程并持续丰富知识库。各阶段通过互连的反馈循环进行迭代精炼。

Result: 在BIRD基准上，Reflect-SQL实现72.03%的执行准确率，显著优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 通过集成多层自我反思机制，Reflect-SQL有效弥合了用户意图与复杂数据之间的鸿沟，展示了在企业级应用中高可靠Text-to-SQL的重大进展。

Abstract: Democratizing data access through natural language is a crucial goal for modern enterprises, but the practical adoption of Text-to-SQL is critically hindered by real-world complexities: 1. Obscure and large database schemas, 2. Ineffective retrieval of relevant tables and columns due to structured setting of schemas and vague user query, 3. Generation of syntactically or logically flawed SQL due to a lack of robust validation and correction mechanism. To address these systemic challenges, we introduce Reflect-SQL, a novel framework for Text to SQL, grounded in multi-stage self-reflection approach to develop understanding of obscure schema using a knowledge base, setup a process for effective retrieval and system to generate syntactically/semantically SQL. Instead of a single-pass attempt, our system employs an LLM-as-a-judge driven scoring mechanism within interconnected feedback loops to iteratively refine the results at every stage. A feedback-driven retrieval loop refines the user's natural language query, while a synthesis loop validates and corrects the SQL and finally, an entailment loop optimizes the end-to-end process and continuously enriches the knowledge base. By integrating these layers of reflection, Reflect-SQL bridges the critical gap between user intent and complex data. On the challenging BIRD benchmark, our framework achieves an execution accuracy of 72.03%, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrating a major leap in reliability for enterprise applications.

</details>


### [87] [UniCon: A Unified Context-Centric Modeling Paradigm for CTR Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03290)
*Jiajun Cui,Zhengqi Xu,Fan Zhang,Zhangteng,Gu Tang,Honghong Zhu,Mengxi Wu,Yulin Liang,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出UniCon，一种统一上下文中心建模架构，将用户历史行为和当前请求视为同构的上下文单元（而非传统异构信号），通过上下文内注意力捕获单元内局部耦合、上下文间注意力建模决策状态动态演化，在美团搜索广告上离线AUC提升0.0139，在线RPM提升3.09%、CTR提升2.07%、收入提升2.95%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的统一CTR建模通常将序列信号与非序列信号在token层面区分并异构处理，这种划分源于传统特征工程实践，与底层用户决策过程不对齐。用户行为本质上是同质上下文单元的序列，历史行为与当前请求仅在于结果是否已知的区别。将二者视为异构信号会掩盖决策上下文中的结构依赖，限制缩放效率和预测质量，尤其在电商货架、瀑布流等上下文丰富场景中问题更为突出。

Method: UniCon将请求上下文作为基本建模单元，把历史行为和预测目标组织为同质上下文单元。具体地，采用上下文内注意力（intra-context attention）捕获上下文内物品间的局部耦合（Locality），以及上下文间注意力（inter-context attention）建模跨上下文的决策状态动态演化（Dynamics）。同时引入上下文单元级别的序列压缩以降低部署开销。

Result: 在美团搜索广告场景中，UniCon相对强生产基线离线AUC提升0.0139；上线后获得统计显著的在线增益：RPM提升3.09%、CTR提升2.07%、收入提升2.95%。

Conclusion: 以上下文为基本单元的统一建模方式弥合了历史与目标之间的结构差距，与用户实际决策过程更对齐，能够更有效地支持统一CTR模型规模化扩展，同时保持较低的部署成本。

Abstract: Unified modeling has become a major direction for industrial click-through rate (CTR) prediction. Existing approaches typically unify sequential and non-sequential signals at the token level, model their interactions in a shared backbone, and increase model capacity to improve scaling behavior. However, this division originates from legacy feature-engineering practice and is misaligned with the underlying decision process. User behavior is inherently a sequence of homogeneous context units; at the level of input organization, historical behavior and the current request differ only in whether their outcomes are observed or remain to be predicted. Treating them as heterogeneous signals obscures structural dependencies within the user's decision context, limiting both scaling efficiency and prediction quality. This limitation is particularly pronounced in context-rich scenarios such as e-commerce shelves and waterfall feeds. To address this, we propose UniCon, a unified context-centric modeling architecture that treats the request context as the basic modeling unit and organizes history and prediction targets as homogeneous context units. Intra-context attention captures local coupling among items within a context (Locality), while inter-context attention models the dynamic evolution of decision states across contexts (Dynamics). This organization bridges the structural gap between history and target and supports more effective scaling of unified CTR models. Context-unit-level sequence compression further reduces deployment overhead. On Meituan search advertising, UniCon improves offline AUC by 0.0139 over a strong production baseline and achieves statistically significant online lifts of 3.09% in RPM, 2.07% in CTR, and 2.95% in revenue.

</details>


### [88] [DoPR: Reusable Compressed Document Prefixes for Efficient LLM Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03311)
*Beiya Dai,Yifan Wei,Guang Yang,Xing Shi,Xinbing Wang,Zhouhan Lin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DoPR框架，通过将文档离线处理为压缩的前缀状态并在在线重排时复用，解耦文档处理与查询重排，在保持约97-99.5% NDCG@10精度的同时实现最高8倍文档端内存减少和8.04倍延迟加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型作为pointwise重排器时，其全量重新处理文档的方式导致不同查询对同一文档进行重复的文档侧计算，存在大量的冗余计算开销，限制了在线重排的效率。

Method: DoPR框架：1) 离线阶段选择与查询无关的文档表示，转换为压缩的文档前缀状态并预计算存储；2) 在线重排阶段仅处理查询和评分token，通过存储的前缀状态提供文档信息，实现文档端压缩与跨查询前缀状态复用。

Result: 在TREC DL、BEIR和BRIGHT基准上使用Qwen3模型（0.6B到8B）进行实验，DoPR实现了最高8.0倍的在线文档端内存减少和8.04倍的延迟加速，同时保持了匹配的全文档重排器平均NDCG@10的97.1%-99.5%。

Conclusion: DoPR通过将文档处理与在线重排解耦并通过压缩前缀状态复用文档信息，有效消除了pointwise重排中的冗余文档侧计算，在保持高重排精度的同时显著提升了在线推理效率，证明了该框架的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are effective rerankers, but pointwise reranking repeatedly processes the same document across different queries, causing substantial redundant document-side computation. We propose \textbf{DoPR}, a compressed document prefix framework that decouples offline document processing from online reranking. DoPR first selects query-independent document representations and converts them into compressed document prefix states, which are precomputed offline and reused whenever the document is retrieved. During online reranking, the model scores each query-document pair by processing only the query and scoring token, with document information supplied by the stored prefix states. This design reduces online cost through both document-side compression and cross-query prefix-state reuse. Experiments on TREC DL, BEIR, and BRIGHT with Qwen3 models from $0.6$B to $8$B show that DoPR achieves up to 8.0$\times$ online document-side memory reduction and up to 8.04$\times$ latency speedup, while retaining \textbf{97.1\%-99.5\%} of the average NDCG@10 of matched full-document rerankers.

</details>


### [89] [SelfDR: Self-Distillation from Reasoning for LLM-Based Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03313)
*Chumeng Jiang,Jiayin Wang,Xinjie Lin,Zhiqiang Guo,Hengliang Luo,Min Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SelfDR是一个基于自蒸馏推理的LLM推荐框架，通过让同一个基座LLM先作为教师生成推理增强的推荐预测，再将其蒸馏给学生模型进行直接推荐，从而在保持推理效率的同时提升推荐准确性。该框架所有组件基于同一LLM，无需外部模型，并在三个公开数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM推荐系统虽然能力强大，但引入显式推理过程会带来高昂的计算成本，限制了其在实际推荐系统中的部署。现有方法需要在推荐准确性和推理效率之间做出权衡，SelfDR旨在解决这一矛盾，通过蒸馏推理增强的预测来实现高效的直接推荐。

Method: SelfDR框架包含教师和学生两个角色，但均基于同一个基座LLM。教师端通过以下游推荐性能为奖励训练一个reasoner，生成针对性的推理链并融入教师输入，从而产生推理增强的预测；学生端则在同一底层模型上学习直接推荐，通过动态加权策略从教师输出中进行自蒸馏，实现从推理增强预测到直接推荐的迁移。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的大量实验验证了SelfDR框架的有效性、合理性和效率。实验表明该方法在不引入外部模型的前提下有效提升了推荐的准确性，同时保持了推理阶段的效率优势。

Conclusion: SelfDR通过自蒸馏框架成功地将LLM的推理能力迁移到直接推荐路径，在不牺牲推理效率的前提下提升了推荐效果，为LLM推荐系统的实际部署提供了可行方案，代码已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful backbones for recommendation. To better elicit their capabilities, reasoning has been widely incorporated to help LLMs interpret rich textual signals and improve recommendation accuracy. However, explicitly generating intermediate reasoning traces often incurs substantial computational costs, which limits practical deployment in real-world recommender systems. To address this challenge, we propose SelfDR, a Self-Distillation from Reasoning framework for LLM-based Recommendation. SelfDR distills an LLM's own reasoning-enhanced predictions to produce recommendations directly, improving recommendation effectiveness while maintaining inference efficiency. All components in the framework are built on the same base LLM, without relying on any external models. Specifically, the teacher recommender is constructed by training a reasoner with downstream performance as the reward, enabling it to generate targeted rationales that are later incorporated into the teacher's input. A student recommender for direct recommendation, with the same underlying model, then learns from the teacher through self-distillation with a dynamic weighting strategy. Extensive experiments on three public datasets validate the effectiveness, rationality, and efficiency of SelfDR. Codes are available at https://github.com/JiangDeccc/SelfDistillation.

</details>


### [90] [SciLENS: RL-Driven Autonomous Agents for Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03338)
*Leqi Zheng,Jinbo Su,Yuying Li,Chaokun Wang,Weiping Wang,Haitao Li,Jiajun Zhang,Shannan Yan,Zhaolu Kang,Rong Fu,Jie Wu,Fang Niu,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: SciLENS是一个完全本地化的自主科学文献综合代理框架，基于约1200万条学术记录的双层基础设施，首次将结构可视化集成到推理循环中，并通过自动化数据合成管线（基于20个前沿模型的跨模型共识）进行训练。在六个科学基准测试中，SciLENS显著优于开源基线，性能与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的科学文献综合代理严重依赖专有在线服务，导致可复现性受限、隐私泄露风险以及无法离线部署。作者旨在开发一个完全本地的自主代理框架来解决这些问题，同时消除对人工标注训练的依赖。

Method: 1) 构建双层基础设施，索引约1200万条学术记录；2) 将结构可视化作为推理循环中的可操作工具，将复杂的引文拓扑压缩为经过验证的数据驱动图表，缓解宏观综合过程中的上下文耗尽问题；3) 开发自动化数据合成管线，从引文知识图谱中提取多跳子图，并通过20个前沿模型的跨模型共识进行验证；4) 采用逆向分解评分策略（reverse-decomposition rubric）提供细粒度的过程奖励，用于早期规划和严格的证据接地，从而对agent进行对齐训练。

Result: 在覆盖标准问答、引文准确性、事实推理和结构综合的六个科学基准评估中，SciLENS显著优于开源基线，并达到与GPT-5.2和Gemini-3.0-pro相当的性能水平。代码和数据已在GitHub公开。

Conclusion: SciLENS证明了一个完全本地的科学文献综合agent可以达到与顶级专有商业模型相当的性能，通过结构可视化和自动化数据合成等创新方法，在保持可复现性、隐私性和离线部署能力的同时，弥合了开源与专有系统之间的性能差距。

Abstract: Scientific literature synthesis agents increasingly rely on proprietary online services, limiting reproducibility, privacy, and offline deployment. To address this challenge, we introduce SciLENS Scientific Localized Evidence Navigation and Synthesis), a fully local autonomous agent framework operating on a dual-tier infrastructure indexing approximately 12 million academic records. SciLENS pioneers the integration of structural visualization as an actionable tool within the reasoning loop, enabling the agent to compress complex citation topologies into validated data-driven charts and thereby mitigate context exhaustion during macro-level synthesis. To train the agent without human annotation, we develop an automated data synthesis pipeline that extracts multi-hop subgraphs from a citation knowledge graph, verified by cross-model consensus among 20 frontier models. The agent is subsequently aligned through a reverse-decomposition rubric strategy that provides fine-grained process rewards for early planning and strict evidence grounding. Evaluations across six scientific benchmarks encompassing standard QA, citation accuracy, factual reasoning, and structural synthesis demonstrate that SciLENS significantly outperforms open-source baselines and achieves performance comparable to GPT-5.2 and Gemini-3.0-pro. Our source code and data are released at https://github.com/LQgdwind/SciLENS.

</details>


### [91] [HypRQ-VAE: Hyperbolic Item Indexing for Long-Tail-Aware Generative Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03369)
*Longfeng Wu,Tong Zeng,Giovanni Seni,Zhimin Peng,Bhanu Pratap Singh Rawat,Si Zhang,Yao Zhou,Lecheng Zheng,Bo Ji,Yujun Yan,Dawei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出HypRQ-VAE，首个在双曲空间中学习物品索引的框架，用于解决生成式推荐系统中LLM文本令牌与离散物品索引之间的失配问题，以及现有欧几里得空间方法难以建模真实目录长尾分布的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐系统利用LLM将推荐建模为语言生成任务，但LLM基于文本令牌而推荐系统依赖离散物品索引，二者存在根本性失配导致幻觉。现有方法在欧几里得空间学习物品词汇表，无法处理真实目录的幂律式长尾分布——头物品占主导而大量尾物品反映用户小众偏好，需要能够自然容纳这种层次和稀疏结构的空间来编码物品。

Method: 引入HypRQ-VAE（Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder），利用双曲几何的指数体积膨胀特性来匹配用户-物品交互的幂律结构，在双曲空间中学习物品索引，从而在编码丰富文本语义的同时保持稀疏长尾物品的表征保真度。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的实验表明，HypRQ-VAE显著提升了推荐性能，尤其在尾物品推荐上效果突出。分析将增益归因于双曲空间在建模物品层次结构和稀疏性方面的优越能力。

Conclusion: 双曲空间是生成式推荐系统中比欧几里得空间更合适的物品索引学习空间，能有效缓解长尾物品建模问题，并提升推荐的精度和泛化能力。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems model user behavior as item ID sequences, while recent generative methods cast recommendation as a language modeling task using large language models (LLMs). While this paradigm incorporates rich textual semantics, it introduces a fundamental mismatch: LLMs operate on text tokens, whereas recommender systems depend on discrete item indices. This misalignment often leads to hallucinations in generative recommendations. Existing methods attempt to bridge this gap by learning item vocabularies in Euclidean space, but they struggle to model the inherent long-tail distribution of real-world catalogs, where a small number of head items dominate, and a vast number of tail items reflect users' niche preferences. To address this issue, we introduce Hyperbolic Residual-Quantized Variational AutoEncoder (HypRQ-VAE), the first framework to learn item indexing in hyperbolic space. HypRQ-VAE leverages the unique properties of hyperbolic geometry, whose exponential volume expansion naturally accommodates the power law structure of user-item interactions. This allows the model to encode rich textual semantics while preserving the representational fidelity of sparse, long-tail items. Experiments on three benchmark datasets show that HypRQ-VAE significantly improves the performance of recommendation, particularly in recommending tail items. Our analysis attributes these gains to the superior capacity of hyperbolic space to model item hierarchies and sparsity in generative recommendation. Our code and data are available at: https://github.com/wulongfeng/HypRQ-VAE.

</details>


### [92] [Plan Pointers and Record-Directive Form in Budgeted Verification of Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03450)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本论文探讨了在单轮智能体（仅继承六行记忆、可拉取至多一条归档记录）中，通过存储中的指令（指针、识别标准或二者结合）如何引导智能体的检索选择。研究在12个注册研究、一个仪器谱系、共14,760次尝试中进行。关键发现包括：六个直接提供商模型上长度匹配的判据比裸ID高+35.0点（研究D），但在OpenRouter的九模型面板上未通过注册优越性规则（研究E）；在Claude模型上追加ID会取消判据效果（Opus 5从40/40降至0/40，研究F-x）；六次字节匹配编辑使每个精确字符串具有独立效果（研究G）；重新运行80次/单元后30个复制对比中15个在边际内、15个未解决（研究G'）。一份批准行（+96.0点）和两份信贷预算在所有三个Claude模型上恢复了目标（研究J）。所有结果仅为固定面板上精确编辑的描述性效应，带注册区间，无机制声明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在检索式智能体（如使用内存存储的agent）中，存储中嵌入的指令（指针、判据/标准、或二者结合）能否以及如何可靠地引导智能体拉取目标记录。核心动机在于检验这些"提示注入式"引导手段在不同模型、不同面板和不同重复条件下的稳健性与边界条件，特别是在注册研究框架下进行严格的可重复性评估。

Method: 方法涉及注册的对照实验：在智能体存储中写入不同形式的指令（裸ID指针、长度匹配的判据、ID+判据组合、批准行、预算指令等），在固定面板（多个直接提供商模型及OpenRouter服务模型，含Claude Opus家族和Fable等）上记录智能体检索请求的目标选择。通过精确编辑字符串逐字节变化、控制运行次数（如80次/单元）、不同存储等多个维度测量选择比例的差异，并使用注册的优越性规则和置信区间评估统计显著性。

Result: 结果包括：判据在直接提供商模型上显著优于裸ID（+35.0点），但未在更广网格面板上复制；追加ID会取消判据（Claude Opus 5 从40/40降至0/40）；批准指令和两信贷预算可恢复目标选择（+96.0点）；判据的取消效应在多数（非全部）措辞和模型上一致，但不同存储下的结果变化。计划指针效应在修正后首次报告（+78.0）与前瞻注册复跑（+81.7）间一致。30个复制对比中，15个在边际内、15个未解决、无超出者。

Conclusion: 结论是：这些指令引导效应在具体配置下是真实且可复现的，但其方向、大小和存在性对模型、措辞、存储环境及是否追加ID等细节高度敏感。没有任何普适性机制被断言——所有报告均严格限定为固定面板上精确字符串编辑的描述性效应，叠加注册区间，不主张通用机制解释。

Abstract: An agent that inherits six one-line memories may pull at most one archived source record before acting; a directive written into the store can steer that choice: a pointer to the record, a criterion that identifies it, or both. Across twelve registered studies on one instrument lineage (14,760 attempts) we measured where the request goes under each form. On six direct-provider models a length-matched criterion exceeded a bare id by +35.0 points [+31.2, +38.8] (Study D); the contrast failed its registered superiority rule on a nine-model OpenRouter-served panel (Study E). Appending the id cancelled the criterion on three Claude models (Opus 5: 40/40 to 0/40; Study F-x); six byte-matched edits gave each exact string its own effect (Study G), and a re-run at eighty runs per cell left fifteen of thirty replication contrasts within the margin, fifteen unresolved and none beyond (Study G'). A ratification line (+96.0 points on Opus 5) and a budget of two credits restored the target on all three (Study J); across five criterion strings the suffix's cancellation held for four of the five wordings on Opus 5 and all five wordings on Fable 5.1 (Study H2); in a second store every model followed the criterion (Study H1). Continued into a decision, the criterion moved the choice toward the current record (+100.0 points, Opus 5) and away from it on Fable 5.1 (Study I). A one-character plan pointer's effect (+78.0 points; Study B, after a correction of its first repository report) returned the same verdict under a prospectively registered re-run (+81.7 points; Study B'). All results are descriptive effects of exact edits on fixed panels with registered intervals and no mechanism claim.

</details>


### [93] [ExplainRoute: A Pre-Deployment Audit Framework for Non-Answer-Giving Programming Tutors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03470)
*Yiming Gai,Yingying Zhang,Xuefei Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ExplainRoute是一个面向"不给答案"式编程辅导系统的部署前审计框架。它估算学习者的解释状态，并自适应地选择Feynman式自我解释提示或苏格拉底式脚手架两种有界响应，通过机器可检查的契约暴露状态、策略、引用代码片段和泄露风险。在1,770对SelfCode语料上的离线评估表明，契约有效性达100%，但自适应路由相比固定策略并无可靠优势。

其核心贡献是一个经过验证的审计协议和一个边界发现（boundary finding），而非改进学习效果、保持率或因果教学有效性的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编程辅导系统应该支持学习者自主解释而非直接给出模型答案。然而现有基准仅按流畅度对辅导系统排名，缺乏对信息边界、响应极性、失败闭合度以及与学习者解释可见性相关价值的系统性审计手段，因此需要在部署前对非作答型辅导系统建立结构化审计框架。

Method: 提出ExplainRoute预部署审计框架，其核心机制包括：(1) 估计学习者解释的当前状态；(2) 在两种有界响应（Feynman式自我解释提示或苏格拉底式脚手架）中选择；(3) 通过机器可检查契约暴露状态、策略、被引用的代码片段与泄露风险。评估采用SelfCode语料库（1,770对），按代码组划分，443对保留在11个未触及的测试组中。对比条件包括：直接答案、固定开放式自我解释、固定苏格拉底脚手架、自适应路由、以及无状态的自适应消融。

Result: 所有教学条件下的契约有效性均达100%。自适应路由在60.5%的记录上与冻结参考规则一致，状态宏F1为0.238（Open: 0.229; Socratic: 0.246），表明无可靠的自适应优势。独立语言模型评判给自适应响应评分为4.516/5，优于无状态消融（2.819/5），但略低于固定开放式自我解释（4.598/5）和苏格拉底脚手架（4.658/5）。对40行分层子集的盲评rubric评估证实，可见的学习者解释能提升信息价值，但自适应路由并未优于固定策略。

Conclusion: 论文贡献在于：(1) 一个经过实证验证的预部署审计协议，可系统性地核查非作答型编程辅导系统的信息边界与响应安全性；(2) 一项边界发现——在当前设计下自适应路由并未带来超越固定策略的可靠优势，且状态预测能力有限。作者明确指出，本研究不提供关于学习改善、知识保持或因果教学有效性的证据，为后续工作划定了清晰的范围。

Abstract: Programming tutors should support learners' own explanations rather than immediately providing model answers. We present ExplainRoute, a pre-deployment audit framework for non-answer-giving programming tutors. Given a code line and a learner explanation, it estimates the explanation state and selects one of two bounded responses: a Feynman-style self-explanation prompt or a Socratic scaffold. The framework exposes its state, strategy, cited code fragment, and leakage risk through a machine-checkable contract. Unlike benchmarks that rank tutors by fluency alone, ExplainRoute audits information boundaries, response polarity, failure closure, and the value of learner-explanation visibility before classroom deployment. We evaluate it offline on the 1,770-pair SelfCode corpus using a code-group split, with 443 pairs reserved in 11 untouched holdout groups. The evaluation compares direct answers, fixed open self-explanation, fixed Socratic scaffolding, adaptive routing, and an adaptive no-state ablation. Contract validity reaches 100% for all pedagogical conditions. Adaptive routing matches the frozen reference rule on 60.5% of records, with state macro-F1 of 0.238 (Open: 0.229; Socratic: 0.246), showing no reliable adaptive advantage. An independent language-model judge scores adaptive responses 4.516/5, outperforming the no-state ablation (2.819/5) but slightly below fixed open self-explanation (4.598/5) and Socratic scaffolding (4.658/5). A blinded rubric evaluation on a stratified 40-row subset confirms that visible learner explanations improve information value while adaptive routing does not outperform fixed strategies. The contribution is a validated audit protocol and a boundary finding, rather than evidence of improved learning, retention, or causal instructional effectiveness.

</details>


### [94] [From Topical Relevance to Answerability: Entailment Distillation for Conversational Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03482)
*Shuai Qin,Guojia An,Weikang Guo,Pei Ke,Jiwei Wei,Yang Yang,Jie Zou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CLEAR框架，将对话式检索从主题相关性转向可回答性（answerability），通过蕴含蒸馏和基于段落的溯因召回模块，在TopiOCQA、QReCC和TREC CAsT数据集上显著提升了检索精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式检索器通常将主题相关性作为可回答性的代理，但紧密匹配对话上下文的段落并不一定是支持正确答案的段落，存在系统性的可回答性差距（answerability gap）。

Method: CLEAR框架包含两个核心模块：(1) 蕴含蒸馏（entailment distillation）：将答案-段落的蕴含监督转移至交叉编码器重排序器，使重排序器在推理时无需答案即可区分支持答案的段落与主题干扰项；(2) 基于段落的溯因召回模块（passage-centric abductive recall）：利用LLM从段落中推断可回答的查询，将低相似度但可回答的段落纳入候选池。

Result: 在TopiOCQA、QReCC以及域外TREC CAsT数据集上，CLEAR在top-ranked precision上一致优于强大的查询改写和密集检索基线，在主题噪声较重的对话中提升最大；在LLM驱动的查询改写器之上应用该重排序器可获得进一步的增益。

Conclusion: CLEAR有效弥合了对话式检索中主题相关性与可回答性之间的差距，证明了基于可回答性的检索范式比传统主题相关性检索更有效，且在噪声对话场景中优势更为明显。

Abstract: Existing conversational retrievers commonly treat topical relevance as a proxy for answerability. However, a passage that closely matches the dialogue context is not necessarily the one that supports the correct answer. We identify this mismatch as a systematic answerability gap. To address this issue, we propose CLEAR, a framework that shifts conversational retrieval from topical relevance to answerability. The core of CLEAR is entailment distillation, which transfers answer-passage entailment supervision into a cross-encoder reranker so that the reranker discriminates answer-supporting passages from topical distractors at inference time, without requiring answers. CLEAR is complemented by a passage-centric abductive recall module that brings low-similarity yet answerable passages into the candidate pool by inferring answerable queries from passages with an LLM. Across TopiOCQA, QReCC, and out-of-domain TREC CAsT datasets, CLEAR consistently improves top-ranked precision over strong query-rewriting and dense-retrieval baselines, with the largest gains observed in conversations involving heavier topical noise. Moreover, applying our reranker on top of an LLM-driven query rewriter yields further gains.

</details>


### [95] [EPIC: Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning for Semantic ID Diffusion Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03522)
*Tuan-Binh Tran,Thanh Tam Nguyen,Quoc Viet Hung Nguyen,Dung D. Le,Tung Kieu,Thanh Trung Huynh*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出EPIC方法，在语义ID（SID）生成式推荐的去噪过程中显式引入物品级竞争：基于当前生成上下文和用户近期交互构建候选物品的后验分布，并投影回未解析的SID位置以引导后续token决策。预训练骨干保持冻结，无需额外decoder前向传播，在四个Amazon基准上取得一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SID掩码扩散推荐方法在每一步去噪时主要依赖位置维度的token预测推理，但部分SID可能对应多个可行物品，缺乏显式的物品级选择竞争机制，导致推荐精度受限。

Method: EPIC通过两个核心步骤：（1）利用当前生成上下文和用户近期交互构建个化的候选物品后验分布；（2）将该分布投影回未解析的SID位置，作为后续token生成的条件信号。整个过程中预训练骨干冻结，无需额外的decoder前向传播开销。

Result: 在四个Amazon基准数据集上，EPIC超越多个强基线模型，取得一致效果提升。诊断分析表明，性能提升主要来源于个性化的转移证据（transition evidence），该机制在去噪过程中保留了有前景的物品假设。

Conclusion: 将物品级显式竞争引入SID去噪过程是有效的，能够在不增加推理开销和保持预训练模型冻结的条件下显著提升生成式推荐的准确性。这为掩码扩散推荐方法提供了一种新的推理范式。

Abstract: Semantic ID (SID) generative recommendation predicts the next item by generating a short tuple of discrete tokens. Recent masked-diffusion methods improve this process through bidirectional context and flexible decoding, yet recommendation ultimately requires selecting among complete catalog items. At each denoising step, a partial SID can correspond to multiple feasible items, while existing methods primarily reason through position-wise token predictions. We propose Explicit Posterior Item Conditioning (EPIC), which introduces explicit item-level competition into SID denoising. EPIC constructs a personalized posterior over feasible candidate items using the current generation context and the user's recent interactions, then projects this distribution back to unresolved SID positions to guide subsequent token decisions. The pretrained backbone remains frozen and requires no additional decoder forward pass. Experiments on four Amazon benchmarks show consistent improvements over strong baselines, while diagnostic analyses indicate that the gains primarily arise from personalized transition evidence that preserves promising item hypotheses during denoising.

</details>


### [96] [LLM4AIGQ: LLM-based AI Guidance Query Generation Framework for Multi Interest Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03674)
*Xiangchen Pan,Jiayi Xu,Jing Wang,Xing Fang,Lingyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM4AIGQ，一种基于大语言模型的AI引导查询生成方案。该方案通过整合用户画像和历史交互序列对用户兴趣进行细分，为每个子兴趣推断消费意图并生成对应的引导查询。在训练上采用SFT+RL+DPO的后训练管线并引入多层次奖励设计，部署上采用近线生成与在线读取的架构，在离线评估和在线A/B测试中均表现稳健。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AI生成查询方法采用"查询到AI生成查询"(Q2AIGQ)的两阶段范式：先从用户画像、历史行为、物品信息等多路径召回主搜索查询，再通过规则方法泛化生成AIGQ。该方案存在两方面局限：一是信息级联丢失导致语义漂移；二是主搜索查询推导过度依赖"用户-物品"共现关系，缺乏对用户多兴趣的探索，导致引导查询价值低且与购买意图不匹配。

Method: 提出LLM4AIGQ框架：(1) 结合用户画像与历史交互序列对用户兴趣进行细分，为每个子兴趣推断具体消费意图并生成对应AIGQ；(2) 采用包含SFT、RL和DPO的后训练管线增强模型生成能力；(3) 引入多层次奖励设计以满足多目标优化和长链推理需求；(4) 部署上采用近线生成与在线读取的架构以满足延迟约束。

Result: 广泛实验表明，该模型在离线评估和在线A/B测试中均取得了稳健的性能表现。

Conclusion: 通过LLM驱动的多兴趣细分与后训练管线，有效克服了传统共现检索方法在表达能力和语义准确性上的局限，为电商场景下引导查询生成提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Guidance queries stimulate user consumption by extracting preferences to provide search queries with guidance value, playing a crucial role in the e-commerce field. Traditional AI-generated queries (AIGQ) generation primarily relies on a two-stage "Query-to-AI-Generated-Query" (Q2AIGQ) association paradigm, first recalling user primary search queries from user profiles, historical behavior sequences, item-side information, and the current query through multi-path retrieval, then generalizing AIGQ via rule-based methods. This approach suffers from semantic drift due to information cascade loss; additionally, primary search query derivation heavily depends on "user-item" co-occurrence relationships, lacking exploration of user multi-interests, resulting in guidance queries with low value and mismatched purchase intent. To address the expressive limitations of traditional co-occurrence-based retrieval, we propose LLM4AIGQ, an LLM-based solution for generating AI guidance queries tailored to users' multi-interests. This approach segments user interests by integrating user profiles and historical interaction sequences, infers specific consumption intents for each sub-interest, and subsequently generates corresponding AIGQ. In terms of model training, we employ a post-training pipeline comprising Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL), and Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) to enhance the model's capability in generating AIGQ. We also introduce a multi-level reward design to satisfy the requirements of multi-objective optimization and long-chain reasoning in practical applications. Regarding deployment, we adopt a nearline-generation and online-read architecture to meet latency constraints. Extensive experimental analyses demonstrate that our model achieves robust performance in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests.

</details>


### [97] [Unified Pitch Graphs for Diagnosing Pitching Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03810)
*Kichang Lee,JeongGil Ko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出统一投球图（UPG），一种用于棒球投球策略回顾性分析的分层图表示方法，在保留每个投球的精确三维轨迹与上下文的同时，通过支撑自适应机制在不同语义和时间分辨率下组织事件。在394万MLB Statcast投球上的实验表明，该方法将保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，并将执行重建R²从0.495提升至0.685。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 棒球中的投球策略不仅体现在物理执行上，还体现在投球使用的有序上下文中，但常见的表示方法将投球简化为离散类型或聚合统计，丢失了精确的空间-时间事件信息和序列上下文，无法捕捉策略中的有序结构。

Method: 提出统一的投球图（UPG）分层图表示方法：①将每次投球保留为带有重建三维轨迹和上下文信息的精确事件；②通过有向序列边连接连续投球；③在语义和时间分辨率上组织相同事件；④设计支撑自适应机制，当重复证据不足时从精细长序列退避，同时保留精确事件谱系。

Result: 在2021至2026年间394万个MLB Statcast投球数据上评估：名义上相同的投球序列表现出不同的物理执行；有序结构在更长上下文条件下愈加明显。支撑自适应退避将保留路径覆盖率从18.9%提升至94.9%，执行重建R²从0.495提升至0.685；UPG还能可靠定位离散投球组合和序列表示无法检测的受控执行变化。

Conclusion: UPG提供了一种可追溯、多尺度的表示方法，用于识别重复出现的策略模式，同时避免将回顾性关联与因果性或未来表现声明相混淆。

Abstract: Pitching strategy in baseball is expressed through both physical execution and the ordered context in which pitches are used, yet common representations collapse pitches into discrete types or aggregate statistics. We present Unified Pitch Graphs (UPG), a hierarchical graph representation for retrospective analysis of sequential spatiotemporal events. UPG preserves each pitch as an exact event with reconstructed three-dimensional trajectory and context, connects consecutive pitches through directed sequence edges, and organizes the same events across semantic and temporal resolutions. A support-adaptive mechanism backs off from fine, long sequences when repeated evidence is insufficient, while retaining exact event lineage. We evaluate UPG on 3.94 million MLB Statcast pitches from 2021 to 2026. Nominally identical pitch sequences exhibit distinct physical executions, and ordered structure becomes increasingly evident in longer context-conditioned paths. Support-adaptive backoff increases held-out path coverage from 18.9\% to 94.9\% while improving execution reconstruction from $R^2=0.495$ to $0.685$. UPG also reliably localizes controlled execution changes that discrete pitch-mix and sequence representations cannot detect. These results demonstrate that UPG provides a traceable, multi-scale representation for identifying recurring strategy patterns without conflating retrospective associations with causal or future-performance claims.

</details>


### [98] [GRASP: Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline for Label-Free Multi-Topic Essay Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03857)
*Aafreen Husain,Samar Shailendra,Saad Sajid Hashmi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了GRASP（图检索自动评分流水线），用于对无标签的多主题科学考试简答题进行自动评分。该方法通过Sentence-BERT编码参考答案构建FAISS向量索引和语义相似度图，利用句子计数启发式和大语言模型判断学生文章中涵盖的题目数量，然后结合RAG和GRAG进行参考答案检索，最后用匈牙利算法实现参考节点与问题段的最优分配，并基于GPT-4.1-mini独立评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史上自动简答题评分研究仅聚焦于单一主题的考试，而对包含多个主题的考试进行自动评分尚未被充分探索。特别是"无标签式"考试（学生将多个问题的回答合并为一段文字，无标记或分段说明）的自动评分更具挑战性，需要解决分段、检索和评分等一系列问题。

Method: GRASP流水线包含以下步骤：(1) 将每个问题的参考答案通过Sentence-BERT编码到FAISS向量索引中，并基于参考答案集合构建语义相似度图；(2) 在评分阶段，先用句子计数启发式结合大语言模型（用于解决模糊情况）预测学生文章中回答的不同主题数量，此过程无需训练数据或领域特定示例；(3) 通过余弦相似度RAG和GRAG检索候选参考节点（GRAG以顶部余弦匹配为种子节点，在强边之间执行图遍历以发现RAG遗漏的参考节点）；(4) 使用匈牙利算法对每个问题段最优分配一个参考节点（不重复）；(5) 用GPT-4.1-mini对各段与其分配的参考独立评分。

Result: 实验旨在展示检索质量对评分准确性的影响，以及在不同文章复杂度水平下图增强检索相比纯余弦相似度方法的优势。

Conclusion: GRASP通过图检索增强的RAG方法提升了多主题无标签科学考试自动评分的检索覆盖率和评分准确性，在处理无训练数据、无领域示例的真实场景中具有应用潜力。

Abstract: Automated short-answer grading research has historically focused on exams consisting solely of questions pertaining to a single topic. Automatic grading of exams containing questions about more than one topic remains less explored. In this work, a Graph-Retrieval Automated Scoring Pipeline (GRASP) is introduced for grading label-free multi-topic science exams. Label-free exams are short-answer exams in which a student's responses to several distinct topics are merged into a single paragraph, with no markup labels or segmentation indicating which span answers which question. Reference answers for each question are encoded into a FAISS vector index via Sentence-BERT, and a semantic similarity graph is constructed over this set of reference answers. At grading time, sentence count heuristics, with a large language model used to resolve ambiguous cases, are first applied to predict how many distinct topics were answered in the student essay. This process is performed without training data or domain-specific example essays. Candidate reference nodes, each storing one (question, reference answer, concatenation of both) from the reference index, are then retrieved through cosine similarity based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GRAG). GRAG operates by taking the top cosine matches as seed nodes and then performing a graph traversal over strong edges to find additional reference nodes that may have been missed by RAG. The Hungarian algorithm is then used to optimally assign one reference node per question segment such that no reference is duplicated. Each segment is then graded against its assigned reference independently using GPT-4.1-mini. This experiment is performed to show the effect of retrieval quality on grading accuracy and the benefit of graph-augmented retrieval versus strict cosine similarity methods at various levels of essay complexity.

</details>


### [99] [Comparing Retrieval Methods for Academic Advisor Discovery: A Six-Method Study of 768 CS Faculty Profiles Across 9 US Universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03901)
*Biraj Subedi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文对六种信息检索方法在"学术导师发现"任务上进行了对比评估（根据研究生申请者的研究兴趣陈述对CS教师进行排序），包括稀疏词法匹配（Jaccard、TF-IDF、BM25）、稠密语义检索（all-MiniLM-L6-v2）、混合分数融合及学习排序。基于新建的领域特化评测集（9个US CS系768份教师档案、5个查询162条相关性判断），Reranked方法取得最高NDCG@10（0.477）。经Bonferroni校正后仅TF-IDF显著劣于BM25、Semantic、Hybrid和Reranked。字段消融发现仅用传记字段性能更优，受控实验表明拼接arXiv论文摘要会降低NDCG，因此推荐晚期融合架构。代码、爬虫和相关性标签均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术顾问（导师）匹配是研究生申请过程中的关键环节，但缺乏系统化的信息检索方法比较研究。作者旨在针对学术顾问发现这一特定任务，建立领域专属评测数据集，并对多种IR范式（从词法匹配到学习排序）进行公平、严格的对比评估，填补该任务在评测资源和方法学验证上的空白。

Method: 构建了包含768份来自9个美国CS系教师档案的领域特化评测集，由5个代表不同研究生科研画像的查询产生162条0/1/2分级相关性判断。对比六种方法：Jaccard重叠、TF-IDF、BM25（稀疏词法）、all-MiniLM-L6-v2句嵌入（稠密语义）、混合分数融合以及学习排序（Reranked）。统计检验采用Bonferroni校正的15组两两比较；另进行字段消融（传记 vs 传记+研究标签）以及受控实验（拼接arXiv论文摘要对性能的影响）。

Result: Reranked获得最高平均NDCG@10（0.477±0.138），其后依次为Semantic（0.450）、Hybrid（0.421）、BM25（0.406）、Jaccard（0.303）和TF-IDF（0.246）。经Bonferroni校正，仅在5个查询上TF-IDF显著逊于BM25、Semantic、Hybrid和Reranked，其余两两差异未通过校正。字段消融显示仅用传记（NDCG 0.634）优于融合研究标签的完整模型（0.593）；受控实验中拼接arXiv摘要使NDCG@10降低0.176。

Conclusion: 学习排序在学术顾问发现任务上表现最佳，但统计显著性有限；稠密语义检索与混合方法具有竞争力，而TF-IDF在同类词法方法中最弱。传记字段是信息量最丰富的信号，简单拼接外部论文摘要文本反而损害性能，支持采用晚期融合（late-fusion）架构设计。该工作为学术导师推荐提供了可复现的基线评测集和方法基准。

Abstract: We present a comparative evaluation of six information retrieval methods for the task of academic advisor discovery: ranking CS faculty members by relevance to a graduate applicant's research interest statement. The methods span sparse lexical matching (Jaccard overlap, TF-IDF, BM25), dense semantic retrieval (all-MiniLM-L6-v2 sentence embeddings), hybrid score fusion, and learning-to-rank. Evaluation uses a new domain-specific collection: 768 faculty profiles scraped from 9 US CS departments, with 162 graded relevance judgments (grade 0/1/2) across 5 queries representing distinct graduate student research profiles. Across all five queries, Reranked achieves the highest mean NDCG@10 (0.477, std 0.138), followed by Semantic (0.450), Hybrid (0.421), BM25 (0.406), Jaccard (0.303), and TF-IDF (0.246). After Bonferroni correction across all 15 pairwise comparisons, TF-IDF is significantly worse than BM25, Semantic, Hybrid, and Reranked; no other pairwise difference survives correction at 5 queries. A field ablation reveals that biography alone (NDCG 0.634) outperforms the full model combining biography with research area tags (0.593). A controlled experiment shows that concatenating arXiv paper abstracts reduces NDCG@10 by 0.176, motivating a late-fusion architecture. All code, scrapers, and relevance labels are released openly.

</details>


### [100] [The WebKurator.de Platform: Combined Regional and Topical Web Curation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03971)
*Michael Dinzinger,Natanael Arndt,Ben Böck,Jelena Mitrović,Michael Granitzer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文介绍WebKurator.de，一个用于德国网络区域与主题联合策展的协作平台。它提出二维策展模型，明确分离主题分类与地理标注，利用LLM主题分类和基于版权的地址提取配合地理编码实现自动化，并从554万网站（其中314万含版权页，258万位于德国且有主题标签）的德国版权数据集引导启动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 国家图书馆和记忆机构在策展网络上仍面临挑战。现有基于目录的方法（如Curlie）采用主题中心的一维层级结构，将地理方面与主题和语言类别交织在一起，无法有效支持区域相关内容的策展需求。

Method: 提出WebKurator.de平台，采用二维策展模型，将主题分类与地理标注显式分离。集成LLM主题分类和基于版权页（imprint）的地址提取与地理编码技术，并支持用户建议和审核机制。平台从包含554万网站的德国版权数据集（German Imprints Dataset）引导构建。

Result: 从554万网站中，314万包含版权页，成功提取并地理编码了邮政地址。其中258万（85.17%）位于德国且具有主题标签，这些网站构成WebKurator.de的初始基础，并可通过用户建议持续扩展。

Conclusion: WebKurator.de通过引入二维策展模型解决了传统目录方法在地理维度上的不足，充分利用版权页数据实现大规模自动化标注，为区域性与主题性网络策展提供了可行方案，同时保持用户协作的开放性。

Abstract: The systematic curation of the Web remains a central challenge for national libraries and memory institutions that aim to preserve culturally and regionally relevant content. Existing directory-based approaches such as Curlie implement a predominantly topic-centric, one-dimensional hierarchy, where geographic aspects are intertwined with topical and linguistic categories. To address this limitation, we present WebKurator.de, a collaborative platform for combined regional and topical web curation, initially focused on the German web. WebKurator introduces a two-dimensional curation model that explicitly separates topical categorization and geographic annotation. The system integrates LLM-based topic classification and imprint-based address extraction with geocoding, and supports user suggestions together with moderated review. The platform is bootstrapped from the German Imprints Dataset, a large-scale collection of 5.54 million websites. Among them, 3.14 million contain imprint pages, for which we successfully extracted and geocoded postal addresses. Of these, 2.58 million (85.17%) are located in Germany and also have an assigned topic label. These websites form the initial foundation of WebKurator.de and can be continuously extended through user suggestions.

</details>


### [101] [The Dice Roll Method: A Standardized Protocol for Repeated-Query Auditing of Large Language Model Brand Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04047)
*Dmitrij Żatuchin*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出"骰子掷法"(Dice Roll Method)作为重复查询审计大语言模型品牌推荐的可复用标准协议，基于温度缩放核采样生成模型，将总响应方差分解为采样、提示措辞、运行间和模型版本等组成。通过负二项混合模型、Cliff's delta、保留依赖的bootstrap和概化理论分解等方法，对五个品牌推荐审计研究（约19万观测、270+品牌、6种语言）进行再分析，得出探索性(n=5)、验证性(n=10)和严格性(n=15)三档迭代次数建议。外部验证在39个单元中有37个重现成功，确认了协议的统计可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者越来越多地使用重复相同的提示来审计LLM品牌推荐中的随机变异，但缺乏标准化的协议来设定迭代次数、选择稳定性指标或建立可靠性阈值。本文旨在形式化一个可复用的统计协议，填补这一方法论空白。

Method: 协议核心组件包括：(1) 将总响应方差分解为采样、提示措辞、运行间和模型版本四个分量；(2) 以迭代次数为重复测量的负二项混合模型；以Cliff's delta为无分布效应量；(3) 保留依赖结构的bootstrap；(4) 基于仿真的功效分析；(5) 概化理论分解；(6) 基于固定快照的漂移诊断。通过D-study推得三档迭代指南，并对四个度量族（计数、集合、嵌入、公平调整PASOR）构建精简指标组合。

Result: D-study得出三档迭代指导：探索性n=5(G=0.58)、验证性n=10(G=0.74)、严格性n=15(G=0.81)。四个度量族彼此互补，支持紧凑度量电池而非单一指标。预注册外部验证（Motoki等人100轮、Rozado 24个模型、llm-stability三个语料库）在39个单元中有37个重现D-study可靠性预测且无失败，n=5时功效值精确到小数点后两位；但固定档位不可迁移，支持"先试点后定案"(pilot-then-solve)的解读。

Conclusion: 该协议为LLM品牌推荐的重复查询审计提供了在真实自回归生成的条件下、非高斯结构下的统计学原则基础。关键在于固定的迭代档位不能随意迁移，最佳做法是先进行试点研究确定所需迭代数，再正式求解。

Abstract: Background: Researchers increasingly use repeated identical prompts to audit stochastic variation in large language model (LLM) brand recommendations, yet no standardized protocol exists for setting iteration counts, selecting stability metrics, or establishing reliability thresholds. Objective: We formalize the Dice Roll Method as a reusable protocol for repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations, grounded in a generative model of temperature-scaled nucleus sampling. Methods: Total response variance is decomposed into sampling, prompt-phrasing, run-to-run, and model-version components. The stack: a negative-binomial mixed model with iterations as repeated measures; Cliff's delta as the distribution-free effect size; dependence-preserving bootstrap; simulation-based power; a generalizability-theory decomposition; drift diagnostics on pinned snapshots. We reanalyse five brand-recommendation auditing studies: approximately 190,000 observations, 270+ brands, 6 languages, iteration counts 5 to 40. Results: Three tiers of iteration guidance emerge from the D-study: exploratory (n = 5, G = 0.58), confirmatory (n = 10, G = 0.74), and rigorous (n = 15, G = 0.81), tied to effect-size and generalizability targets. The four metric families (count, set, embedding, fairness-adjusted PASOR) are complementary, motivating a compact metric battery over single indicators. A pre-registered external validation on three independent corpora (Motoki et al., 100-round; Rozado, 24 models; llm-stability) reproduces the D-study reliability prediction in 37 of 39 cells with no failures and the n = 5 power value to two decimals; the fixed tiers do not transfer, supporting a pilot-then-solve reading. Conclusion: The protocol gives repeated-query auditing of LLM brand recommendations a statistically principled footing under the conditional, non-Gaussian structure of real autoregressive generation.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一种图网络学习模拟器，通过将交互历史直接存储在接触边上的持久化记忆中来模拟颗粒动力学。在2D和3D颗粒柱崩塌基准测试中，TRACE相比GNS和NMGNS将长滚动位置误差降低31-62%、最终沉积误差降低58-89%，同时使用更少参数，并比MPM参考求解器分别快了12.2倍和8.9倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒动力学严重依赖颗粒间的接触历史，而接触的形成、破裂和重排使得历史信息难以保留。现有图网络模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，未能充分捕捉沿接触边传递的历史交互信息，导致长时程预测精度不足。

Method: TRACE将交互历史直接存储在接触边上，每条边维护一个由注意力机制消息传递和门控循环单元(GRU)更新的持久化记忆；同时引入边身份字典来在接触图动态变化时保留边的记忆。物理结构化解码器预测法向和切向接触力，强制执行库仑摩擦极限，并施加大小相等方向相反的内力。训练采用单步预训练和自回归滚动微调两阶段策略。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱崩塌基准上，TRACE产生稳定、物理一致的长时程滚动，精确复现最终沉积几何和动能释放。相比GNS和NMGNS，长滚动位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，且参数量更少、颗粒互渗透接近零。相比MPM参考求解器，2D和3D分别获得12.2倍和8.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 将交互历史存储于接触边上的设计显著优于节点级记忆方案，验证了边缘级记忆对颗粒接触动力学建模的有效性，为高效、高精度学习型颗粒模拟器提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [103] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型在不确定时的预测由其unembedding矩阵中一个编码训练语料unigram分布的方向主导（称为"无知方向"），该方向出现在Llama、Qwen、Gemma和Pythia四个模型家族中。将最终预测状态投影到此方向得到逐token的"先验加载因子λ"，该因子随上下文信息量增加而下降，并与tempered Bayesian更新（unigram先验指数为λ与上下文似然的乘积）精确对应。λ经过几何-概率校准后可在模型间比较，且该方向具有因果活性——调整λ可引导预测偏离或趋近unigram先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解语言模型在缺乏足够上下文信息时如何做出预测，揭示其内部隐式编码的概率先验结构及其几何来源，从而建立模型预测机制与贝叶斯推理之间的可解释桥梁。

Method: 在四种模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia，0.4B至405B参数）的unembedding矩阵中识别编码unigram分布的单一方向；通过将最终预测状态投影到该方向提取每个token的先验加载因子λ；证明该投影将预测状态分解为两个正交向量，对应tempered Bayesian更新（unigram先验^λ × 上下文似然）的两个因子；通过干预实验验证该方向的因果作用。

Result: 无知方向在全部四族模型（0.4B~405B参数）中一致存在；λ随上下文信息量增加稳定下降，且经校准后可在不同模型间可比；更大模型在高上下文条件下通常表现出更低的先验依赖；通过机械干预拉高或拉低λ可以在KL散度层面将预测朝或远离unigram先验引导，证明该方向因果活跃。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测状态在几何上编码了一种tempered Bayesian结构：unigram先验的方向性投影解释了模型在低信息情境中的行为，λ作为可量化的先验依赖强度的桥接指标，兼具可解释性与干预可控性，为理解和控制语言模型的不确定性决策提供了新视角。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [104] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出"方程重铸"(equation recast)方法，将参数化算子学习转化为单一典范算子学习，通过从控制方程中解析推导参数引起的算子变化并将其吸收为有效源项，实现跨新参数域的零样本预测。该方法在多变参数、非线性和奇异PDE场景中支持外推，能将稀疏异构数据集整合到共享典范表示中，并以收敛失败作为内部警告信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化模型在广泛参数范围内学习解算子需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数的数据，且模型可能在训练分布之外静默失效。这限制了神经PDE求解器在参数外推和新物理场景中的泛化能力与可靠性。

Method: 方程重铸方法将参数化算子学习重铸为学习单一典范算子。参数引起的算子变化从控制方程中解析推导得到，并将其吸收为有效源项，从而在典范域中统一不同参数下的求解任务，实现零样本预测。

Result: 在多变参数、非线性和奇异PDE环境中验证了外推能力和异构稀疏数据整合；在核聚变的高保真托卡马克仿真中，通过典范域映射在四个设备几何结构上统一了电子温度数据，实现了单一联合训练算子的预测。

Conclusion: 方程重铸为可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了实现路径，结合了方程引导的迁移、数据效率和可监控的推理能力。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [105] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇综述，系统调研了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习（包括联邦学习和去中心化学习），旨在整合这一新兴领域。文章首先回顾了欧几里得数据场景下的基础原理（围绕学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个核心维度），然后将讨论扩展到图结构数据，提出了图分布场景的分类法、刻画了统计异质性、建立了标准化的问题表述和算法框架，最后指出了开放挑战和有前景的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习（单点收集数据、训练和推理）面临可扩展性和隐私等根本性限制。协作学习虽已有大量研究，但绝大多数聚焦于具有规则网格结构（如图像、文本）的欧几里得数据，无法捕捉许多真实世界应用中以图形式表征的关系模式。图学习依赖节点间的消息传递机制，在概念上天然适合需要信息交换的协作环境，但图结构数据在协作环境下的学习机会与挑战仍未被充分探索，因此需要一篇系统性的综述来整合这一新兴领域。

Method: 本文作为综述类论文，采用结构化分析方法：首先回顾欧几里得数据下协作学习的基础原理，从学习有效性、效率和隐私保护三个核心维度进行组织和归纳；随后将讨论延展至图结构数据，提出图分布场景的分类学，刻画不同场景下的统计异质性特征，并据此制定标准化的问题表述和算法框架；最后系统性地梳理开放挑战并展望未来研究方向。

Result: 本文不是提出新算法或报告的实证研究，而是一篇综合性综述。其主要成果包括：提出了一个面向从欧几里得到图结构数据的协作学习统一视角；引入了一种图分布场景的分类方法；明确了图协作学习中统计异质性对学习的影响机制；建立了标准化问题表述和算法分类框架；并系统整理了该领域的开放挑战和未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 协作学习从欧几里得数据向图结构数据的拓展是一个重要且尚未被充分探索的研究方向。本文通过系统性的文献梳理和框架化，为该领域提供了统一的视角和规范化的问题表述，有助于未来研究者在图协作学习方向上进行更深入研究。同时，作者指出该领域仍面临诸多开放的挑战，包括但不限于图分布异质性处理、通信效率和隐私保障等方面。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [106] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于头级功能组织（RFIS与RPD两个干预指标）的证据驱动方法来指导FA和LA混合架构的分配，发现检索头与位置头存在由"Global Positional Band"（GPBand）分隔的完整分类，并据此设计出Head-wise Hybrid Architecture（HwH），在低于1:3的FA/LA比例下同时保留语言建模与推理能力并增强零样本长上下文外推。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的FA和LA混合架构中，两者的分配方式仍是启发式的，缺乏证据支撑。作者希望在RoPE Transformer中学到的头级功能组织中找到合理的分配依据，从而提升混合架构设计的科学性与性能。

Method: 提出两个干预指标：(1) RFIS（RoPE频率重要性分数），测量每个频率对注意力分布的影响；(2) RPD（RoPE位置依赖性），分离对旋转位置调制的依赖。基于这两个指标在Qwen3系列和Llama3.1上验证检索头与位置头的完整分类，并在受控Transformer上确认边界与训练长度位置尺度相关（即GPBand）。据此设计HwH：用NoPE FA处理全局检索，用LA处理局部位置建模，以头粒度进行分配并采用层级特定配置。

Result: 在FA与LA之比低于1:3的情况下，HwH保留了强大的语言建模与常识推理能力，同时改善了检索性能并显著增强了相比Transformer、LA和逐层混合基线的零样本长上下文外推能力。消融实验验证了两个设计原则及各组件的作用。

Conclusion: 头级功能组织分析为混合架构设计提供了实证基础，证明了有原则的混合架构设计是通向未来基础模型的有前景路径，同时提示零样本长度外推失败的一个潜在原因。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [107] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TailRL（尾部似然强化学习），通过直接优化策略在随机奖励阈值下超过该阈值的对数概率，代替传统的期望奖励优化，从而保留对稀有高回报轨迹的概率质量。TailRL只需修改advantage函数即可兼容现有RL流程，在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化等任务中优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习优化平均奖励，但对生成式策略而言，均值会掩盖关键差异：两个策略可能具有相同的平均奖励，但产生稀有高回报rollout的概率却截然不同。随着训练和推理时采样数量的增加，保留高回报结果上的概率质量变得至关重要。

Method: TailRL将连续奖励转化为一族二值成功事件（针对每个奖励阈值，策略超过该阈值的概率），最大化随机选取的奖励阈值被超过的对数概率。其梯度更多地加权稀有高回报的rollout，可解释为Best-of-k梯度的混合。实现上仅需对advantage函数做简单修改即可融入现有RL pipeline。

Result: 在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI grounding和代码优化四类任务上，TailRL能够利用稀有高回报训练样本避免次优解，并且产出的模型在推理时受益于更多额外采样。

Conclusion: TailRL通过直接优化奖励分布的上尾覆盖而非期望奖励，为生成式策略在采样增加场景下提供了更优的优化目标，且其实现简单、兼容性强，展示了显著的实证优势。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [108] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于物理信息的图注意力网络(GAT)代理模型，直接在TCAD四面体网格上预测漂移-扩散方程的基本未知量（静电势与电子/空穴准费米能级），以替代昂贵的3D FinFET TCAD仿真。模型沿用有限体积电流连续性残差作为物理约束，具备跨器件几何与族别的尺寸泛化能力，结合深度集成的不确定性与主动学习实现在线筛选，在大规模多鳍FinFET上达到亚伏级RMSE精度，吞吐量比全仿真高数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散仿真在3D FinFET设计中计算开销极大，随网格复杂度陡增，严重限制了多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习代理模型仅将固定设计参数映射到少量标量指标，丢弃了底层物理，且缺乏跨几何与器件族别的迁移能力。因此需要一种兼顾物理保真度与可迁移性的高效代理模型。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络(GAT)代理：将四面体TCAD网格视为图，在每个网格节点上预测漂移-扩散系统的三个基本未知量（静电势、电子与空穴准费米能级）。训练损失由数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差共同组成，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数。模型可缩放泛化至多鳍栅格；采用深度集成输出逐节点不确定性，驱动主动学习循环快速筛选候选设计并仅将最具信息量的样本送交全仿真。

Result: 在Sentaurus Device基准的多鳍三栅极FinFET上，代理模型对三个漂移-扩散场的逐场RMSE低于伏级，单设计吞吐量比全仿真高数个数量级。优势随器件尺寸增大而增强：对于直接仿真过慢的大型多鳍阵列，推理仍可在每器件亚秒内完成，支持在器件尺度上探索直接TCAD扫描不可行的Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: 该物理信息图注意力网络代理模型有效弥合了TCAD仿真精度与机器学习速度之间的鸿沟，通过直接在网格上预测场变量并嵌入物理残差，实现了跨尺寸的高效泛化与主动学习驱动的大规模设计空间探索，为先进FinFET器件的多目标优化开辟了实际可行的路径。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [109] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLIWBO方法：从有限输入映射库中选择warp函数以适配输入几何，在保留GP-UCB高概率收敛保证的同时加速贝叶斯优化，尤其适用于输入坐标与真实几何不匹配的场景（如对数缩放超参数、局部峰值）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-BO在HPO和MAS设计中表现优异，但传统GP-UCB的收敛保证要求固定核函数。实际应用中原始坐标往往与目标函数的真实几何不匹配（如超参数需要对数缩放、存在局部峰值），输入warping可大幅提升采样效率，但已知GP-UCB证明无法处理动态变化的warp。

Method: FLIWBO从有限库中选取平滑输入映射（warp），允许任意历史依赖的选择规则。在温和假设下推导出高概率收敛保证，库大小的代价为显式的√(N_ε)。通过诊断实验识别几何失配类型与失败案例，并在多个基准上进行验证。

Result: 在扭曲合成目标、confidenence-fence陷阱和Fashion-MNIST HPO四个重复基准上，FLIWBO-UCB在几何失配场景中超越原始坐标GP-UCB；能逃脱甚至击败oracle-warp EI的陷阱；恢复手动对数缩放的大部分增益；在允许匹配遗憾保证的方法中表现领先。20维MAS设计研究验证了代价昂贵噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO弥合了输入warping与GP-UCB收敛保证之间的理论鸿沟，证明有限库warp选择既能动态适配几何又可保留理论保障，为实际应用中的几何失配问题提供了可行且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [110] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子航海图（ENC）中的空间对象表示为图结构（对象为节点，空间与语义关系为边），将新旧ENC数据集编码为一对图，并将ENC变更分类任务建模为图对分类问题，用于判断变更对航行安全构成关键还是非关键风险。实验对比多种GNN架构和配置，结果表明基于图的表示能有效提升ENC更新分类的准确性，为ENC维护流程的自动化提供了可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ENC是用于海上导航的地理空间矢量数据集，其维护挑战较大，尤其是根据对航行安全的重要性对ENC中对象变更进行分类尤为关键。然而，现有方法尚不清楚哪种消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示最适合该分类任务。

Method: 将矢量导航数据集表示为图结构，空间对象作为节点，对象间的空间和语义关系构成边；将旧版和新版ENC数据集分别编码为一对图，并将任务定义为图对分类问题。基于此表示，训练并评估多种GNN架构与模型配置。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于图的表示能够提升ENC更新的分类准确率，为自动化或改进ENC维护工作流程提供了可扩展的方法。

Conclusion: 图表示与GNN架构在ENC变更分类任务中表现出有效性，可作为自动化ENC维护流程的可行解决方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [111] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出教师门控式在线策略蒸馏（TGOPD）方法，在提示词层面验证教师模型的可靠性后再决定是否采用密集监督信号进行蒸馏，从而避免不可靠教师带来的错误引导，并在多个领域与模型规模上超越基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）通过教师模型对学生自身rollout提供密集token级监督来加速后训练，但vanilla OPD对所有提示词统一施加监督，未检查教师在每个提示词上是否可靠。由于reverse KL具有mode-seeking特性，一个自信但错误的教师会产生强而误导的更新。已有的分布性代理指标（如熵或师生似然一致性）只能衡量不确定性或一致性，无法直接验证结果正确性，因此需要在提示词层面直接验证教师可靠性。

Method: TGOPD基于"教师可靠性应在允许密集监督之前于提示词级别被验证"的原则构建。该方法利用一小部分由验证器评分（verifier-scored）的教师探针来估计教师可靠性，并根据可靠性检查结果对每个提示词进行路由：当可靠性检查通过时将提示词专门分配给密集OPD，否则分配给基于验证器得分的GRPO。此外，TGOPD利用了异步OPD中原本空闲的教师计算能力来进行可靠性估计。

Result: 在4B和35B学生模型上，涉及数学、代码和指令跟随任务，TGOPD在所有六个单领域设置中均优于Vanilla OPD，并且在多领域训练下于两个规模上均取得更高的七个基准平均分。在异步OPD中，TGOPD将教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%（在4B单领域测量运行中），减少了教师侧计算浪费。

Conclusion: TGOPD通过提示词级别的教师可靠性门控，有效避免了不可靠教师对蒸馏过程造成的误导性更新，在多个领域和模型规模上一致优于vanilla OPD，同时大幅提升了异步蒸馏中教师节点的计算利用率，展示了可靠性与效率兼顾的蒸馏方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [112] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对"因果基础模型"(Causal Foundation Models, CFMs)这一新兴领域进行了实践性介绍。CFMs将基础模型的范式引入因果推断，它们是通过预训练得到的神经网络，能够在新数据集上通过上下文学习直接估计因果量（如平均处理效应）而无需微调模型参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断针对每个新问题都需要定制流程：提出因果机制、选择合适的估计器并训练，效率低下且缺乏泛化能力。与此同时，机器学习多个领域已转向基础模型范式——在大规模数据上预训练一次，即可应用于新任务而无需微调。本文旨在将这一成功范式引入因果推断，推动因果推断效率和可扩展性的提升。

Method: 这是一篇综述/教程性质的文章，而非提出新方法。作者首先总结了因果推断和机器学习所需的背景知识，然后系统性地介绍因果基础模型的定义、预训练方式及其通过上下文学习处理全新数据集的工作机制，并配有示例代码和Jupyter笔记本以便读者实践。

Result: 文章提供了一个关于因果基础模型的全面实践性指南，涵盖了该领域所需的知识框架，并通过可运行的代码示例帮助读者理解和应用CFMs。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型代表了因果推断领域向基础模型范式的转变，使平均处理效应等因果量的估计能够在全新数据上通过上下文学习直接完成，无需针对每个新问题重新训练模型，具有重要的研究与应用前景。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [113] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench是一个基准框架，用于评估可解释性方法产生的内部估计器（observer）在驱动干预、控制或安全任务时是否真正充分。它区分了估计准确性与行动损失，在多个模型（GPT-2-small、Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it等）和任务上证明了二者可能不一致，并提供了固定任务合同、可运行基线和基于表格的提交机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性机制（mechanistic interpretability）日益被用于指导干预，如激活引导、电路移除和安全监控。然而，一个平均意义上准确的内在估计仍可能导致次优行动。现有评估往往只报告估计准确性，未检验该估计器在它所指导的实际任务（干预、控制或安全）中是否足够充分，这是一个关键的评估盲区。

Method: 论文提出ObserverBench基准框架：每个任务固定模型、信息边界、允许行动、决策规则、留出案例和损失函数。基准分别报告估计准确性与所选行动导致的损失。针对闭环控制任务，理论分析了观察者误差在起始点及干预可达方向上如何影响行动质量。在电路干预任务（GPT-2-small、Qwen2.5-7B）中对比成对观察者与基于行动损失的观察者；在安全分流任务中，用AUROC与部署损失对比排序，并比较不同信息源与SAE读出头的表现。

Result: 实验表明：（1）成对观察者在预测未见效应上更准确，但未必总是选择更好的行动；基于行动损失训练的观察者能选择更低损失的干预。（2）安全分流中，能完美分离违规的得分在违规成本不同时可能错误分配固定干预预算。（3）在Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it及冻结的Qwen3.5-9B APPS任务上，AUROC对监控器的排序与部署损失排序不一致，且最佳信息来源因模型而异。（4）稀疏SAE读出在报告的Qwen面板上落后于层匹配的密集对照。

Conclusion: ObserverBench强调不能仅用估计准确性评估可解释性方法，必须通过其启用的实际行动质量来衡量。该框架提供固定任务合同、可运行基线和表格提交机制，为可解释性方法在干预、控制与安全场景中的充分性评估建立了标准化流程。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [114] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM提出将神经算子的预训练和迁移学习联合设计：先在小子域上预训练神经算子，对于新的设置冻结该算子，仅训练一个轻量模块来组合局部预测，从而降低目标域的部署成本。在两个互补问题（稳态Darcy流和大攻角俯仰翼型非定常流）上验证，相比全尺寸模型的适应方法，误差降低36-56%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子是物理模拟的快速可微替代模型，但当域几何、尺寸或工况偏离训练分布时精度会严重下降。虽然监督式适应可以恢复精度，但即使很小的目标集也需要昂贵的高保真仿真。因此需要将预训练和迁移学习协同设计，以降低部署成本。

Method: LatentDDM采用两阶段策略：(1) 在小子域上预训练神经算子以预测局部物理场；(2) 面对新设置时冻结该算子，仅训练一个轻量模块将局部预测进行组合。这种方法将预训练与"组合层面的迁移"共同设计，避免了在全域上重新适应大模型的高成本。

Result: 在稳态Darcy流中，相比处理全域的容量匹配模型，LatentDDM在更大的域上仅用16个目标仿真进行适应后误差降低36-56%；在快速俯仰翼型非定常流中，LatentDDM改善了20步场滚动的精度，在零样本和少样本校准后均有效。

Conclusion: 协同设计的局部预训练与组合层级迁移，是构建物理基础模型（physical foundation models）的一种有前景的设计原则，能够在域间泛化中显著降低对新场景的适应成本。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [115] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream是一个面向端侧大语言模型（LLM）推理的流式"推测-精化"框架。它通过在部分GPU结果可获取时渐进式精化计算、加载和缓存保留的优先级，实现GPU执行与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，解决了设备端推理中稀疏执行决策需最新上下文与高效计算-I/O重叠需早期预测之间的矛盾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
